אצבע יתירה – this is the high finger–digit (i.e., talon) that is behind the [other] fingers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:6:3)


**וְקֻרְקְבָנוֹ נִקְלָף.** הַכִּיס שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַקֻּרְקְבָן נִקְלָף מִבְּשַׂר הַקֻּרְקְבָן. וְלֹא שֶׁיִּהְיֶה כָּל עוֹף טָהוֹר צָרִיךְ לְכָל הַסִּימָנִים הַלָּלוּ, אֶלָּא סִימָן אֶחָד בִּלְבָד דַּי, וּבִלְבָד שֶׁנֵּדַע בּוֹ שֶׁאֵינוֹ דוֹרֵס וְאוֹכֵל. וְהַגְּאוֹנִים כָּתְבוּ שֶׁמָּסֹרֶת בְּיָדָם שֶׁאֵין מַכְשִׁירִים עוֹף הַבָּא בְּסִימָן אֶחָד וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיָּדוּעַ לָנוּ בּוֹ שֶׁאֵינוֹ דוֹרֵס וְאוֹכֵל, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הָיָה אוֹתוֹ סִימָן שֶׁקֻּרְקְבָנוֹ נִקְלָף בַּיָּד, אֲבָל אִם אֵינוֹ נִקְלָף בַּיָּד אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ זֶפֶק אוֹ אֶצְבַּע יְתֵרָה מֵעוֹלָם לֹא הִתִּירוּהוּ:


וקרקבנו נקלף – the pocket that is within the thick muscular stomach–craw peals from the flesh of the thick muscular stomach–craw . But not that every pure–clean fowl has all of these organs, but one organ alone is sufficient, but as long as we know about it (i.e., the animal) that it doesn’t attack with paws or claws and eat. But the Gaonim wrote that the tradition in their hands was that they don’t validate fowl that comes with one organ and even though it is known to us regarding it that it does not attack with paws or claws and eats, other than if that that organ whose thick muscular stomach can be pealed by hand, but if it cannot be peeled by hand even though it always has a crop or an extra finger–talon, they did not permit it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 3:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:6:4)


**הַחוֹלֵק אֶת רַגְלָיו.** כְּשֶׁמַּעֲמִידוֹ עַל הַחוּט נוֹתֵן שְׁתֵּי אֶצְבְּעוֹתָיו מִכָּאן וּשְׁתַּיִם מִכָּאן:


החולק את רגליו – when he places it on a chord–sinew, it places its two fingers from there and two from there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 3:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:6:5)


**טָמֵא.** בְּיָדוּעַ שֶּׁהוּא דוֹרֵס:


טמא – whereby it is known that it attacks with its claws or paws.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:7:1)


**וּבַחֲגָבִים.** זֶהוּ סִימַן טָהֳרָתָם:


ובחגבים – this is the sign of their pu- ity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:7:2)


**כֹּל שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ אַרְבַּע רַגְלַיִם וְאַרְבַּע כְּנָפַיִם וְיֵשׁ לוֹ קַרְסֻלַּיִם.** הֵם שְׁנֵי רַגְלַיִם אֲרֻכִּים לְבַד הָאַרְבַּע. וְהֵם סָמוּךְ לְצַוָּארוֹ מִמַּעַל לְרַגְלָיו לְנַתֵּר בָּהֶם, כְּשֶׁהוּא רוֹצֶה לִקְפֹּץ מִתְחַזֵּק בָּהֶם:


כל שיש לו ארבע רגלים וארבע כנפים ויש לו קרסולים (see Leviticus 11:21- jointed legs) – they are two long legs outside of the four. And they are adjacent to its neck, above from its legs to leap with them when it wishes to jump, it strengthens itself with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:7:3)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר וּשְׁמוֹ חָגָב.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה סִימָנִים הַלָּלוּ אֵינוֹ כָשֵׁר, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יָדוּעַ שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ חָגָב. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


רבי יוסי אומר ושמו חגב – even though it has these four distinguishing marks, it is not kosher, other than if it is known that its name is locust. And this is the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:7:4)


**כֹּל שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ סְנַפִּיר וְקַשְׂקָשֶׂת.** אֲפִלּוּ אֵין לוֹ עַכְשָׁיו וְעָתִיד לְגַדֵּל לְאַחַר זְמַן, אוֹ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ עַכְשָׁיו וְעָתִיד לְהַשִּׁירָן כְּשֶׁיּוֹצֵא מִן הַמַּיִם, מֻתָּר:


כל שיש לו סנפיר וקשקשת – even if it doesn’t have it now, and will in the future will grow one after a while, or that it has one now, and it will, in the future will drop it when it leaves the water (see Tractate Hullin 66a), it is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 3:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/3:7:5)


**וּסְנַפִּירִין הַפּוֹרֵחַ בָּהֶן.** שֶׁשָּׁט בָּהֶן עַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם:


וסנפירין הפורח בהם – that roam–swim with them over the face of the water.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:1:1)


**בְּהֵמָה הַמַּקְשָׁה כוּ'.** מֻתָּר בַּאֲכִילָה. הָעֻבָּר כֻּלּוֹ, וַאֲפִלּוּ מְקוֹם חֲתָךְ שֶׁל אֵבָר. דְּאִם הוֹצִיא יָדוֹ וְלֹא הֶחֱזִירָהּ צָרִיךְ לְהַנִּיחַ מִמַּה שֶּׁבִּפְנִים לַצַּד הַחִיצוֹן וּלְחָתְכוֹ, שֶׁמְּקוֹם הַחֲתָךְ הַבְדָּלַת הַחִיצוֹן וְהַפְּנִימִי אָסוּר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא עוֹמֵד עַל שְׂפַת הָרֶחֶם. אֲבָל הֶחֱזִירָהּ אֵין צָרִיךְ לַחְתֹּךְ לְצַד פְּנִים, אֶלָּא מְצַמְצֵם וְחוֹתֵךְ וּמְקוֹם חֲתָךְ מֻתָּר. דְּמַאי טַעֲמָא נֶאֱסַר הָאֵבָר כְּשֶׁיָּצָא לַחוּץ, מִשּׁוּם וּבָשָׂר בַּשָּׂדֶה טְרֵפָה, בָּשָׂר שֶׁיָּצָא חוּץ לִמְחִצָּתוֹ שֶׁהוּא לוֹ שָׂדֶה, טְרֵפָה. מַה טְּרֵפָה כֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּטְרְפָה שׁוּב אֵין לָהּ הֶתֵּר, אַף בָּשָׂר כֵּיוָן שֶׁיָּצָא חוּץ לִמְחִצָּתוֹ שׁוּב אֵין לוֹ הֶתֵּר. וּמְקוֹם חֲתָךְ לֹא יָצָא חוּץ לִמְחִצָּתוֹ, הִלְכָּךְ מֻתָּר כְּשֶׁהֶחֱזִירָהּ קֹדֶם שְׁחִיטָה, דְּקָרִינַן בֵּיהּ בְּהֵמָה בַּבְּהֵמָה תֹּאכֵלוּ:


בהמה המקשה. מותר באכילה – [it is permitted to eat – when the dam is properly slaughtered] the entire fetus and even the place cutting of the limb. For if it put out its hoof–hand and didn’t retracted, one must leave it from what is inside to the outside and to cut it, for the place of the cut you have distinguished the outer part, but the inner part is prohibited, because it stands on the rim of the womb. But if it withdrew it (i.e., its hoof–hand), one does not need to cut to the inner side, but one confines and cuts and the place that one cut is permitted. For what is the reason that the limb is forbidden when it leaves to the outside? Because of (Exodus 22:30): “[You must not eat] flesh torn by beasts in the field.” Meat that left outside of its compartment which for it is a field, is “torn.” Just as something that is “torn,” once it was “torn,” furthermore has no permit, even meat, since it went outside of its compartment. And the place of the cut did not leave outside of its compartment; furthermore, it is permitted when it returns prior to the ritual slaughter, for we call it (Deuteronomy 14:6): “and any other animal [that has true hoofs which are cleft in two and brings up the cud] – such (an animal) you may eat.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:1:2)


**הֲרֵי הוּא כְיָלוּד.** וְתוּ לֹא מַהֲנֵי לֵיהּ שְׁחִיטַת אִמּוֹ, וְצָרִיךְ שְׁחִיטָה לְעַצְמוֹ אִם נִמְצָא חַי. וְאִם נִמְצָא מֵת הֲרֵי הוּא כִנְבֵלָה:


הרי הוא כילוד – and furthermore, he may not benefit from the ritual slaughter of its mother, and he requires a ritual slaughter of its own if he is found living. But if he (i.e., the offspring) is found dead, it is like something that died of itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:1:3)


**חוֹתֵךְ מֵעֻבָּר שֶׁבְּמֵעֶיהָ.** וְהִנִּיחַ הַחֲתִיכָה בְּתוֹכָהּ, מֻתָּר בִּשְׁחִיטַת הַבְּהֵמָה וְלֹא נֶאֱסָר מִשּׁוּם אֵבָר מִן הַחַי:


חותך מעובר שבמעיה – and he left the piece within her, it is permitted with the ritual slaughter of the animal and is not forbidden because of “the limb from a living animal” (one of the seven Noahide commandments).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:1:4)


**מִן הַטְּחוֹל וּמִן הַכְּלָיוֹת.** שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה עַצְמָהּ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:1:5)


**אָסוּר בַּאֲכִילָה.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִנִּיחוֹ בַּבְּהֵמָה לֹא הֻתַּר בִּשְׁחִיטָתָהּ. וּלְהָכִי נָקַט טְחוֹל וּכְלָיוֹת, מִשּׁוּם דְּמִידִי דְלֹא מִטַּרְפָא בְּהוּ הוּא:



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:2:1)


**הַמְבַכֶּרֶת הַמַּקְשָׁה לֵילֵד.** בְּפֶטֶר רֶחֶם שֶׁלָּהּ. מֻתָּר לַחְתֹּךְ אֵבָר אֵבָר כְּשֶׁהוּא יוֹצֵא רִאשׁוֹן רִאשׁוֹן:


מהבכרת המקשה לילד – with the first of her womb. It is permitted to cut it up limb by limb when it comes outfirst.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:2:2)


**וּמַשְׁלִיךְ לַכְּלָבִים.** דְּכָל כַּמָּה דְלֹא נָפַק רֻבֵּיהּ לֹא קַדִּישׁ:


ומשליך לכלבים – for as long as its majority had not left [the womb], it is not holy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:2:3)


**יָצָא רֻבּוֹ.** כְּאֶחָד, וַחֲתָכוֹ:


יצא רובו – as one [complete animal], and [then] he cut it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:2:4)


**הֲרֵי זֶה יִקָּבֵר.** דְּבִיצִיאַת הָרֹב חָלָה קְדֻשָּׁה עָלָיו, דְּקָרִינַן בֵּיהּ אֲשֶׁר יִוָּלֵד:


הרי זה יקבר – for with the exit of most, the sanctity takes effect upon it and we call it (Deuteronomy 15:19): “[You shall consecrate to the LORD your God all male firstlings] that are born [in your herd and in your flock.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:2:5)


**וְנִפְטְרָה מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה.** שֶׁהַבָּא אַחֲרָיו אֵינוֹ בְכוֹר. בֵּין שֶׁיָּצָא רִאשׁוֹן אֵבָר אֵבָר וּבֵין שֶׁיָּצָא רֹב כְּאֶחָד, דְּהָא שֵׁנִי לָאו פֶּטֶר רֶחֶם הוּא:


ונפטרה מן הבכורה – that which comes after him (i.e., the first-born male animal), is not a firstling, hether it first came out limb by limb, or whether the majority of it came out as one, for the second is not the first of the womb.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:3:1)


**בִּבְהֵמָה טְהוֹרָה טָהוֹר.** מִקַּל וָחֹמֶר, וּמָה אִם הוֹעִילָה לוֹ מְחִצַּת אִמּוֹ לְהַתִּירוֹ בַּאֲכִילָה בִּשְׁחִיטָתָהּ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא מֵת, לֹא תוֹעִיל לוֹ כְּשֶׁהִיא חַיָּה לְטַהֲרוֹ מִלְּטַמֵּא. וּבְהֵמָה טְמֵאָה מְנָלָן, דְּאָמַר קְרָא (ויקרא יא) וְכִי יָמוּת מִן הַבְּהֵמָה, זוֹ בְהֵמָה טְמֵאָה. אֲשֶׁר הִיא לָכֶם לְאָכְלָה, זוֹ בְהֵמָה טְהוֹרָה. אִיתְקַשׁ בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה לִבְהֵמָה טְהוֹרָה, מַה בְּהֵמָה טְהוֹרָה עֻבָּרָהּ טָהוֹר, אַף בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה עֻבָּרָהּ טָהוֹר:


בבהמה טהורה טהור – from an a fortiori inference (see Tractate Hullin 70b), if [being inside] its mother is effective to permit it for consumption through its slaughter, even though it (i.e., the fetus) is dead, then should being inside its mother not also be effective to render it pure from the impurity of an animal carcass? And from where do we learn that the same applies regarding an unclean animal? For it states in Scripture (Leviticus 11:39): “If an animal that you may eat has died, anyone who touches its carcass shall be impure until evening.” [The phrase:” “If an animal…. has died,” this is an unclean animal; “that you may eat,” this is a clean–pure animal. A comparison of these juxtaposed “unclean animal” and “clean animal” – just as in a clean–pure animal, its fetus is pure, even in an unclean animal, its fetus is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:3:2)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי אוֹמֵר בִּטְמֵאָה טָמֵא.** וּמַפִּיק לֵיהּ מִקְּרָא דִּכְתִיב (שם ה) אוֹ נֶפֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר תִּגַּע בְּכָל דָּבָר טָמֵא אוֹ בְנִבְלַת חַיָּה טְמֵאָה אוֹ בְנִבְלַת בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה, וְכִי נִבְלַת בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה מְטַמְּאָה וְנִבְלַת בְּהֵמָה טְהוֹרָה אֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה. אֶלָּא אֵיזֶה, זֶה עֻבָּר שֶׁבַּטְּמֵאָה טָמֵא, וּבַטְּהוֹרָה טָהוֹר מִקַּל וָחֹמֶר. וּבַטְּמֵאָה טָמֵא דְּלֵיכָּא קַל וָחֹמֶר וְלֹא דָרְשִׁינַן הֶקֵּשָׁא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי הגלילי אומר בטמאה טמא – and he derives it from Scripture, as it is written (Leviticus 5:2): “Or when a person touches any impure thing -be it the carcass of an impure beast or the carcass of impure cattle,” Does the carcass of an impure beast defile and the carcass of a pure animal does not defile? But rather, which is it {that in an impure animal it defiles and that in a pure animal it doesn’t defile)? This is a [dead] fetus that is in the womb of an impure animal that is impure, and [a dead fetus] that is in a pure animal that is pure, from an a fortiori. But regarding the impure animal, there is no a fortiori [inference], and we don’t expound an analogy based upon biblical inference. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:3:3)


**הַחַיָּה טְמֵאָה טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה.** וּמִדְרַבָּנָן, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יוֹצִיא הָעֻבָּר אֶת רֹאשׁוֹ חוּץ לַפְּרוֹזְדוֹר וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְיָלוּד וּמְטַמֵּא, וְהַחַיָּה סְבוּרָה שֶׁעֲדַיִן הוּא בְּמֵעֶיהָ וְאָתֵי לִטְהוּרֵי. אֲבָל בְּרוֹעֶה שֶׁהוֹשִׁיט יָדוֹ לִמְעֵי בְהֵמָה דְּקָתָנֵי מַתְנִיתִין טָהוֹר, לֵיכָּא לְמִגְזָר הָכִי, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁרֶחֶם שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה גָּלוּי וְכִי מַפִּיק חָזֵי לֵיהּ:


החיה טמאה טומאת שבעה – and it is from the Rabbis, as a decree lest the fetus bring forth its head outside the corridor–the lower end of the vagina or uterus, and he is like he was born and defiles and the midwife thinks that it (i.e., the fetus) is still in her womb and comes to be pure. But the shepherd who inserted his hand into the womb of the animal, as it is taught in our Mishnah is pure. We don’t make this decree, because the womb of the animal is revealed, and when it (i.e., the fetus) comes out, he sees it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:3:4)


**וְהָאִשָּׁה טְהוֹרָה עַד שֶׁיֵּצֵא הַוָּלָד.** דְּאִשָּׁה מַרְגֶּשֶׁת בְּעַצְמָהּ כְּשֶׁמּוֹצִיא רֹאשׁוֹ חוּץ לַפְּרוֹזְדוֹר וְלֹא אָתֵי לְטִהוּרֵי:


ואשה טהורה עד שיצא הולד – for the woman creates excitement for herself when it (i.e., the fetus) brings forth its head outside of the corridor–the lower end of the vagina or uterus, and he does not come to be purified (see Talmud Hullin 72a).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:1)


**הַבָּשָׂר טָהוֹר.** בְּשַׂר הָעֻבָּר טָהוֹר, שֶׁאֵין בְּהֵמָה מְקַבֶּלֶת טֻמְאָה מֵחַיִּים:


הבשר טהור – the flesh–meat of the fetus is pure, for the animal does not receive impurity while living.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:2)


**הַבָּשָׂר מַגַּע נְבֵלָה.** בְּשַׂר הָעֻבָּר מַגַּע אֵבָר מִן הַחַי, שֶׁהוּא מְטַמֵּא כִּנְבֵלָה:


הבשר מגע נבילה – the flesh–meat of the fetus touched a limb from a living animal, which defiles like carrion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:3)


**מַגַּע טְרֵפָה שְׁחוּטָה.** שֶׁהַשְּׁחִיטָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָהּ מַתֶּרֶת הָאֵבָר בַּאֲכִילָה, מְטַהֲרַתּוּ מִידֵי נְבֵלָה. וְהַוְיָא כִּטְרֵפָה שְׁחוּטָה שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה מִן הַתּוֹרָה אֶלָּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן בְּמֻקְדָּשִׁין:


מגע טריפה שחוטה – for the ritual slaughter, even though it does not permit the limb for consumption, purifies it from being carrion, and it is like something torn that is ritually slaughtered that does not defile from the Torah, but rather from the Rabbis with those things that are sanctified [for the Temple]: This is our reading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:4)


**הָכִי גָרְסִינַן, אַף שְׁחִיטַת בְּהֵמָה תְּטַהֵר אֶת הָאֵבָר.** וְלֹא גָרְסִינַן אֶת הָעֻבָּר:


אף שחיטת בהמה תטהר את העבר – and we don’t have the reading “of the fetus.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:5)


**לֹא אִם טִהֲרָה שְׁחִיטַת טְרֵפָה אוֹתָהּ.** מִן הַדִּין הוּא שֶׁהֲרֵי דָּבָר שֶׁגּוּפָהּ:


לא אם טיהרה שחיטת טריפה אותה – this is according to the law, for it is a thing that is part of its body.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:6)


**וּמִנַּיִן לַטְּרֵפָה שֶׁשְּׁחִיטָתָהּ מְטַהַרְתָּהּ.** דְּשֶׁמָּא אֵינָהּ מְטַהַרְתָּהּ וּמִן הַדִּין אֵינָהּ מְטַהַרְתָּהּ, שֶׁבְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה אֲסוּרָה בַאֲכִילָה וּטְרֵפָה אֲסוּרָה בַאֲכִילָה: **וּמַה טְּמֵאָה אֵין שְׁחִיטָתָהּ מְטַהַרְתָּהּ.** מִלְּטַמֵּא, דְּהָכִי תַּנְיָא בְּתוֹרַת כֹּהֲנִים, לְכָל הַבְּהֵמָה אֲשֶׁר הִיא מַפְרֶסֶת פַּרְסָה וְגוֹ', כָּל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהֶם יִטְמָא, לְהָבִיא בְּהֵמָה טְמֵאָה שֶׁלֹּא תְטַהֲרֶנָּה שְׁחִיטָתָהּ:


ומנין לטריפה ששחיטתה מטהרתה – for perhaps it does not purify it, and from the law, does not purify it, for an unclean–impure animal is prohibited for consumption and a torn animal is forbidden for consumption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:7)


**תֹּאמַר בִּטְרֵפָה שֶׁהָיְתָה לָהּ שְׁעַת הַכֹּשֶׁר.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּחָל עֲלָהּ תּוֹרַת שְׁחִיטָה, תּוּ לֹא פָקְעָה מִנַּהּ וְהַוְיָא בִּכְלַל שְׁאָר צֹאן וּבָקָר:


ומה טמאה אין שחיטתה מטהרתה – from being impure, for this is taught in Torat Kohanim (i.e., the Midrash Halakha on the Book of Leviticus – see Leviticus 11:26,24: “among all the animals...that has true hoofs….whoever touches their carcasses shall be impure until evening” – all who touch them shall be impure, to include an unclean animal that its slaughter will not purify it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:8)


**טֹל לְךָ מַה שֶּׁהֵבֵאתָ.** טֹל מִכָּאן רְאָיָה זוֹ שֶׁהֵבֵאתָ:


תאמר בטריפה שהיתה לה שעת הכושר – for since it takes effect the law of ritual slaughtering, furthermore, it does not escape from it for it is within the general realm of the rest of sheep and cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:9)


**הֲרֵי שֶׁנּוֹלְדָה טְרֵפָה מִן הַבֶּטֶן מִנַּיִן.** שֶׁתְּטַהֲרֶנָּה:


טול לך מה שהבאת – take from here this proof that you brought.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:10)


**שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּמִינָהּ שְׁחִיטָה.** הִלְכָּךְ לֹא נַפְקָא מִכְּלַל צֹאן וּבָקָר. אֲבָל בֶּן שְׁמֹנָה חַי שֶׁנּוֹלַד מִבְּהֵמָה חַיָּה אֵין לָנוּ בְּמַה לְטַהֵר אֲפִלּוּ נִשְׁחַט, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ בִכְלַל בָּקָר וָצֹאן. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


הרי שנולדה טריפה מן הבטן מנין – that it would make it pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:11)


**בֶּן שְׁמֹנָה חַי.** אִם נוֹלַד וּשְׁחָטוֹ:


שיש במינה שחיטה – therefore, it does not escape from the generalization of sheep and cattle. But a living animal [fetus] that is eight [months] old that was born from a living animal, we don’t have with what to purify it even it is slaughtered, because it is not included with cattle and sheep. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:4:12)


**אֵין שְׁחִיטָתוֹ מְטַהַרְתּוֹ.** מִידֵי נְבֵלָה, שֶׁאֵין שְׁחִיטָה מוֹעֶלֶת בְּבֶן שְׁמֹנָה, אֶלָּא כְּשֶׁהוּא בִּמְעֵי אִמּוֹ נִתָּר בִּשְׁחִיטַת אִמּוֹ:


אין שחיטתה מטהרתו – from being carrion, for the ritual slaughter of an eight [month] fetus is of any benefit, but rather, when it is in the womb of its mother, it is permitted through the slaughter of its mother (as we will learn in the next Mishnah).



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:1)


**וּמוֹצִיא אֶת דָּמוֹ.** דְּחֶלְבּוֹ בִּלְבָד הוּא דְשָׁרֵי. כִּדְתַנְיָא בְּתוֹרַת כֹּהֲנִים, כְּשֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר חֵלֶב וּשְׁתֵּי כְּלָיוֹת בָּאָשָׁם, שֶׁאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר דְּמִקַּל וָחֹמֶר הֲוָה יָלְפִינַן לָהּ, וּמַה שְּׁלָמִים שֶׁאֵין כָּל מִינָן טָעוּן אַלְיָה, הֲרֵי הֵן טְעוּנִים חֵלֶב וּשְׁתֵּי כְּלָיוֹת, אָשָׁם שֶׁכָּל מִינוֹ טָעוּן אַלְיָה, אֵינוֹ דִין שֶׁיִּטְעֲנוֹ חֵלֶב וּשְׁתֵּי כְּלָיוֹת, וּמַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר, אֶלָּא לוֹמַר לָךְ מַה חֵלֶב וּשְׁתֵּי כְּלָיוֹת הָאָמוּר בָּאָשָׁם מוּצָא מִכְּלַל שְׁלִיל, שֶׁאֵינְךָ יָכוֹל לוֹמַר חֵלֶב שְׁלִיל הַנִּמְצָא בָּאָשָׁם יַקְרִיב, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵין אָשָׁם בָּא נְקֵבָה, אַף כָּל אֲפִלּוּ בְּקָרְבָּנוֹת הַבָּאִים נְקֵבָה, חֵלֶב הָאָמוּר בָּהֶן מוּצָא מִכְּלַל שְׁלִיל. וְכֵיוָן דְּאֵין חֵלֶב שְׁלִיל קָרֵב בְּכָל הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת, שָׁרֵי בַּאֲכִילָה. אֲבָל דָּמוֹ לֹא גָרַע מִדַּם הָאֵבָרִים דְּקַיְמָא לָן בְּמַסֶּכֶת כְּרֵתוֹת דַּם הָאֵבָרִים עוֹבֵר בְּלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה:


ומוציא את דמו – and its fat alone is permitted, as it is taught in Torat Kohanim (i.e., the Midrash Halakha on the Book of Leviticus), when it states, the fat and the two kidneys with a guilt-offering (see Leviticus 7:4: “the two kidneys and the fat that is on them at the loins”), and we don’t need to say that from an a fortiori we would derive it, for just as regarding an offering of well-being–peace offering that anything of their species requires the fat-tail, for they require the fat and two kidneys. The guilt offering, that all of its species requires the tail, is it not the law that it requires the fat and the two kidneys? And what does the inference teach us, but rather to tell us that just as fat and the two kidneys that are stated with the guilt offering is removed from the general category of the embryo, for you are not able to state that the fat of the embryo that is found in a guilt offering should be offered with a female animal, even all, even the sacrifices that come from a female, the fat that is mentioned regarding them one removes from the category of the embryo. But since the fat of the embryo is not offered with all the sacrifices, it is permitted for consumption. But its blood is not worse than the blood of the limbs and we hold that in the Tractate Keritot that the blood of the limbs one violates a negative commandment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:2)


**טָעוּן שְׁחִיטָה.** דָּחֳדָשִׁים גָּרְמֵי לְשַׁוְיֵהּ בְּהֵמָה בְּאַנְפֵּי נַפְשָׁהּ וְלֹא אִתְרַבֵּי מִכֹּל בַּבְּהֵמָה תֹּאכֵלוּ:


טעון שחיטה – for newborns indirectly to compare an animal by itself, and to nt to be included–to be derived by implication with all the cattle that you may eat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:3)


**וְחַיָּב בְּאוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ.** שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁחֲטֶנּוּ בְּיוֹם שֶׁשָּׁחַט אֶת אִמּוֹ:


וחייב באותו את בנו – that you should not slaughter it on the date that you ritually slaughter its mother.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים שְׁחִיטַת אִמּוֹ מְטַהַרְתּוֹ.** דָּחֳדָשִׁים וְלֵידָה גָּרְמֵי:


וחכמים אומרים שחיטת אמו מטהרתו – that the newly born and giving birth is the cause of it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:5)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן שְׁזוּרִי כוּ'.** לְדִבְרֵי חֲכָמִים כֵּיוָן שֶׁהָלַךְ עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע טָעוּן שְׁחִיטָה מִדְּרַבָּנָן, דְּאָתֵי לְאִחְלוֹפֵי לֶאֱכֹל בְּהֵמָה בְּלֹא שְׁחִיטָה. וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן שְׁזוּרִי מַתִּיר אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁהִפְרִיס עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ר' שמעון שזורי כו' – to the words of the Sages, since that it (i.e., the animal) walked on the ground, it requires ritual slaughter according to the Rabbis. For one might exchange it in order to eat an animal without ritual slaughter. Burt Rabbi Shimon Shezuri permits even after he it had a parted hoof on the


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:5:6)


**קְרָעָהּ.** לַבְּהֵמָה בְּלֹא שְׁחִיטָה:


קרעה – [cut into] the animal without ritual slaughter.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:1)


**בְּהֵמָה שֶׁנֶּחְתְּכוּ רַגְלֶיהָ.** הָאַחֲרוֹנִים:


בהמה שנתחכו – the hind–back [legs].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:2)


**מִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה וּלְמַטָּה.** שָׁלֹשׁ עֲצָמוֹת בַּיָּרֵךְ, הַתַּחְתּוֹן הוּא עֶצֶם הַנֶּחְתָּךְ עִם הַפְּרָסוֹת כְּשֶׁמַּפְשִׁיטִין הַבְּהֵמָה וְאוֹתָהּ רְכוּבָה נִקְרֵאת אַרְכֻּבָּה הַנִּמְכֶּרֶת עִם הָרֹאשׁ, וּבִלְשׁוֹן לַעַ"ז קוֹרִין אוֹתוֹ פֶּרֶק יינוקל"ו, וּבַעֲרָבִי דוקב"א. וּלְמַעְלָה הֵימֶנָּה עֶצֶם הָאֶמְצָעִי וְצֹמֶת הַגִּידִים בְּתַחְתִּיתוֹ סָמוּךְ לְפֶרֶק אַרְכֻּבָּה הַנִּמְכֶּרֶת עִם הָרֹאשׁ. וְהָעֶלְיוֹן הִיא קוּלִית הַתְּחוּבָה בָּאַלְיָה. וְהַפֶּרֶק שֶׁבֵּין סוֹף הַקּוּלִית לְרֹאשׁ הָעֶצֶם הָאֶמְצָעִי נִכָּר וְנִרְאֶה בַּגָּמָל כְּשֶׁהוּא רוֹבֵץ יוֹתֵר מִבִּשְׁאָר בַּעֲלֵי חַיִּים. וּמִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה וּלְמַטָּה דְּקָתָנֵי בְּמַתְנִיתִין שֶׁהִיא כְשֵׁרָה הַיְנוּ מִתְּחִלַּת הָאַרְכֻּבָּה הַנִּמְכֶּרֶת עִם הָרֹאשׁ וּלְמַטָּה. וּמִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה וּלְמַעְלָה דְּהַיְנוּ מִסּוֹף הָעֶצֶם הָאֶמְצָעִי שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם צֹמֶת הַגִּידִים, בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁנֶּחְתַּךְ הָרֶגֶל מִשָּׁם וּלְמַעְלָה טְרֵפָה:


מן הארכובה ולמטה – there are three bones in the thigh. The lowest is the bone that is cut with the split hoofs when they flay the animal and this joint is called the knee that is sold with the head, and in the foreign tongue, we call that limb–joint YEENOKLAV, and in Arabic DUKBA. And above it there is the middle bone and the bunch of converging sinews in the thigh (see Talmud Hullin 76a) underneath it adjacent to the limb–joint of the knee that is sold with the head. And the uppermost is the thigh-bone which is inserted in the tail. And the joint which is between the end of the thigh-bone to the top of the middle bone is known and seen in a camel when it lies down more than the rest of the living creatures. And from the knee and below as it is taught in our Mishnah that it is kosher, which is from the beginning of the knee that is sold with the head and below. But from the knee and upward, which is from the end of the middle bone, which is the place of bunch of converging sinews in the thigh, in every place where the foot is cut, from there and above is “torn” (not kosher).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:3)


**וְכֵן שֶׁנִּטַּל צֹמֶת הַגִּידִין.** אֲפִלּוּ לֹא נֶחְתַּךְ הָרֶגֶל שֶׁלֹּא נִשְׁבַּר הָעֶצֶם אֶלָּא שֶׁנִּטַּל צֹמֶת הַגִּידִים, טְרֵפָה. כָּךְ פֵּרְשׁוּ קְצָת אֲמוֹרָאִים מִשְׁנָה זוֹ בַּגְּמָרָא. וְשִׁיטָה זוֹ תָּפְסוּ רַבּוֹתַי עִקָּר וְהוֹרוּ שֶׁבְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁתֵּחָתֵךְ הָרֶגֶל לְמַעְלָה מֵהָאַרְכֻּבָּה הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה שֶׁקּוֹרִין בְּלַעַ"ז ינוקל"ו, בֵּין בִּמְקוֹם צֹמֶת הַגִּידִין בֵּין לְמַעְלָה מִצֹּמֶת הַגִּידִין, טְרֵפָה. אֲבָל רַמְבַּ"ם וְרַב אַלְפַס רַבּוֹ תָּפְסוּ עִקָּר הַפֵּרוּשׁ הָאַחֵר, שֶׁמְּפָרֵשׁ מִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה וּלְמַטָּה כְּשֵׁרָה מִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה וּלְמַעְלָה פְּסוּלָה, דְּהָכִי קָאָמַר, לְמַטָּה מֵאַרְכֻּבָּה הָעֶלְיוֹנָה שֶׁהוּא הַקּוּלִית הַתְּחוּבָה בָּאַלְיָה, וְלֹא לְמַטָּה מִיָּד הַסָּמוּךְ לוֹ אֶלָּא לְמַטָּה מֵעֶצֶם הָאֶמְצָעִי כֻּלּוֹ דְּהַיְנוּ בָּעֶצֶם הַתַּחְתּוֹן, וַדַּאי כְּשֵׁרָה. לְמַעְלָה מִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה דְּהַיְנוּ בַּקּוּלִית, וַדַּאי טְרֵפָה כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּחָתֵךְ. וְכֵן שֶׁנִּטַּל צֹמֶת הַגִּידִין, כְּלוֹמַר, וּבָעֶצֶם הָאֶמְצָעִי יֵשׁ מָקוֹם שֶׁהִיא טְרֵפָה כְּגוֹן בְּצֹמֶת הַגִּידִין, וְיֵשׁ מָקוֹם שֶׁהִיא כְשֵׁרָה כְּגוֹן לְמַעְלָה מִן הַצֹּמֶת. וְאַל תִּתְמַהּ הֵיאַךְ אִי פָּסֵיק לָהּ לְמַעְלָה מִן הַצֹּמֶת בַּעֶצֶם הָאֶמְצָעִי כְּשֵׁרָה, וּכְשֶׁתַּשְׁפִּיל לְחָתְכָהּ בַּצֹּמֶת טְרֵפָה, שֶׁאֵין אוֹמְרִים בִּטְרֵפוֹת זוֹ דּוֹמָה לָזוֹ, שֶׁהֲרֵי חוֹתְכָהּ מִכָּאן וּמֵתָה חוֹתְכָהּ מִכָּאן וְחָיְתָה, וְלֹא נֶאֶסְרָה בְּהֵמָה זוֹ מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא חֲתוּכַת רֶגֶל מִמָּקוֹם זֶה, אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנֶּחְתְּכוּ הַגִּידִין, שֶׁחֲתִיכָתָן הִיא מִכְּלַל הַטְּרֵפִיּוֹת. וּמְקוֹם צֹמֶת הַגִּידִין הוּא מַתְחִיל מִן הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁהֵן נִרְאִים קָשִׁין וּלְבָנִים עַד הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁיַּתְחִילוּ לְהִתְרַכֵּךְ וּלְהִתְאַדֵּם:


וכן שניטל צומת הגידין (if the juncture of the thigh sinews was removed – even if the foot was not cut off that the bone was not broken but the bunch of converging sinews in the thigh were removed, it is invalid–”torn.” So explained some of the Amoraim of the Mishnah in the Gemara (Talmud Hullin 76a-b). But this approach my teachers–Rabbis took hold of as essential and taught that in every place where the leg was cut off above from lower knee which they call Y’NUKLAV in the foreign tongue, whether in the place where the bunch of converging sinews or whether above from the bunch of converging sinews, it is [considered] “torn” (unfit, not kosher). But Maimonides and Rabbi Alfasi his teacher took hold as essential a different commentary–explanation – that from the knee and below is kosher and from the knee and above is unfit, for this is what he said: below from the upper knee which is the thigh-bone that is inserted in the tail, but not below immediately adjacent to it, but rather, below from the middle bone entirely, which is the lower bone, is definitely kosher. Above from the knee, which is the thigh-bone, is definitely “torn” (i.e., unfit, not kosher) in every place that he cut off. And similarly, if he removed the bunch of converging sinews, meaning to say, that in the middle bone, there is a place that is “torn” such as the bunch of converging sinews, and there is a place which is kosher, such as above from the bunch of converging sinew. But don’t be astonished how if he severed it above from the bunch on the middle bone, it is kosher, but when he lowers to cut the bunch, it is “torn,” that we can’t say that this torn part is similar to that one, for if one cuts from here and the animal dies, one cuts from there and the animal lives. And this animal is not forbidden because its leg was severed from this place, but rather because the bunch of sinews were cut, for their severing is within the general [definition] of those things that are torn. And the place of the bunch of the converging sinews begins from the place where they appear hard and white until the place that they begin to soften and redden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:4)


**צֹמֶת הַגִּידִים.** הֵם שְׁלֹשָׁה חוּטִים לְבָנִים בַּבְּהֵמָה צְמוּתִים וּמְחֻבָּרִים יַחַד, אֶחָד עָבֶה וּשְׁנַיִם דַּקִּים. וְאִם נִטַּל הָאֶחָד הֶעָבֶה לְבַדּוֹ אֵין זֶה נִטַּל צֹמֶת הַגִּידִים, שֶׁהֲרֵי נִשְׁאֲרוּ שְׁנַיִם. וְאִם נִטְּלוּ שְׁנַיִם הַדַּקִּים מִמְּקוֹמָם מֻתֶּרֶת, שֶׁהֲרֵי הָאֶחָד הָעָב גָּדוֹל מִשְּׁנֵיהֶם וַהֲרֵי לֹא נִטַּל כָּל הַצֹּמֶת אֶלָּא מִעוּטָם. וְאִם נֶחְתַּךְ רֻבּוֹ שֶׁל כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶן, טְרֵפָה. וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר אִם נֶחְתְּכוּ כֻּלָּן אוֹ נִטְּלוּ כֻּלָּן. וּבָעוֹף הֵם שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר חוּטִים לְבָנִים. אֲפִלּוּ לֹא נֶחְתַּךְ אֶלָּא רֻבּוֹ שֶׁל אֶחָד מֵהֶן, טְרֵפָה:


וצומת הגידין – they are three white sinews in the animal that meet and are connected together. One of them is thick and the [other] two are thin. If the thick one alone is removed, this is not the removal of the bunch of converging sinews, for two of them remain. But if the two think wones are removed from their place, it is permitted, for the one that is thick and larger than the other two, for the entire bunch of converging sinews were not removed, only a minority of them. But if most of each of them were severed, it is “torn” (i.e., not kosher). And one does not need to state if all of them were severed or if all of them were removed. And with fowl, they are sixteen white sinews, even if only the majority of one of them was severed, it is “torn” (i.e., not kosher).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:5)


**נִשְׁבַּר הָעֶצֶם.** לְמַטָּה מִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין עוֹשֶׂה אוֹתָהּ טְרֵפָה:


נשבר העצם – below from the knee in the place where it doesn’t make it “torn” (i.e., not kosher).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:6)


**אִם רֹב הַבָּשָׂר קַיָּם.** דְּהַיְנוּ שֶׁעוֹר וּבָשָׂר חוֹפִים רֹב עָבְיוֹ וְרֹב הֶקֵּפוֹ שֶׁל שֶׁבֶר. דְּזִמְנִין דְּמַשְׁכַּחַת לֵיהּ זֶה בְּלֹא זֶה כְּגוֹן שֶׁמַּרְחִיב מִצַּד אֶחָד וּמֵצֵר מִצַּד אַחֵר, שֶׁאֵין הָעֶצֶם עָגֹל, הִלְכָּךְ בָּעֵי לְתַרְוַיְהוּ:


‘אם רוב הבשר קיים – which is the hide and flesh cover most of its thickness and most of its circumference of the break. For sometimes it is found this without that, such as when it widens from one side and is narrow from the other side, for the bone is not round. Therefore, it requires both.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:7)


**שְׁחִיטָתוֹ מְטַהַרְתּוֹ.** לָאֵבָר הַמְדֻלְדָּל. וּמֻתָּר אֲפִלּוּ בַּאֲכִילָה:


שחיטתו מטהרתו – to the limb hanging down from the body, and it is permitted, even for consumption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:6:8)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁאֵין עוֹר וּבָשָׂר חוֹפִים אֶת רֻבּוֹ, אֵין שְׁחִיטָתוֹ מְטַהַרְתּוֹ. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְהֵמָה מֻתֶּרֶת הֲוֵי אֵבָר אָסוּר מִשּׁוּם וּבָשָׂר בַּשָּׂדֶה טְרֵפָה, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן [דַּף ע"ג] לְהָבִיא אֵבָר וּבָשָׂר הַמְדֻלְדָּלִים. וְאִם נִשְׁבַּר הָעֶצֶם מִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה וּלְמַעְלָה בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה אוֹתָהּ טְרֵפָה, אִם רֹב בָּשָׂר קַיָּם, אֵבָר וּבְהֵמָה מֻתָּר. וְאִם לָאו, אֵבָר וּבְהֵמָה אָסוּר. וְדִין הָעוֹף כְּדִין בְּהֵמָה לְדָבָר זֶה:


ואם לאו – that the hide and the flesh do not cover the majority, the slaughtering does not render it clean. But even though the animal is permitted, the limb is forbidden because of (Exodus 22:30): “you must not eat flesh torn by beasts in the field; [you shall cast it to the dogs],” as we state (Tractate Hullin 73a), to include the limb and flesh hanging down from the body. But if the bone is broken form the knee and above to the place where it makes it “torn,” if most of the flesh exists, the limb and the animal are permitted. But if not, the limb and the animal are prohibited. And the law of the fowl is like the law of the animals of the field for this matter.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:1)


**שִׁלְיָא.** כְּמִין כִּיס שֶׁהָעֻבָּר מֻנָּח בְּתוֹכוֹ:


שליא (placenta–after-birth) – a jind of pocket where the fetus is placed into it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:2)


**נֶפֶשׁ הַיָּפָה.** שֶׁאֵין דַּעְתּוֹ קָצָה בָהּ מֵחֲמַת מִאוּס:


נפש היפה – for it is not counted on in his mind (and therefore forbidden to handle) on account of its repulsiveness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:3)


**תֹּאכְלֶנָּה.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן אֵבָר מִן הַחַי הוּא, אֶלָּא גַם הִיא נִתֶּרֶת בִּשְׁחִיטַת הָאֵם:


תאכלנה – but we don’t say that it is the limb from a living animal (i.e., one of the seven Noahide commandments), but also is a remnant from the ritual slaughtering of the mother [animal].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:4)


**וְאֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה.** דְּלָא חֲשִׁיבָא אֹכֶל:


ואינה מטמאה – as it is not considered food.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:5)


**וְלֹא טֻמְאַת נְבֵלוֹת.** אִם מֵתָה הַבְּהֵמָה:


ולא טומאת נבילות – if the animal died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:6)


**מְטַמְּאָה טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִין.** אִם נָגְעָה בַּטֻּמְאָה, דְּמַחֲשָׁבָה מְשַׁוְיָא לֵיהּ אֹכֶל. אֲבָל טֻמְאַת נְבֵלוֹת לֹא, דְּלָאו בָּשָׂר הוּא אֶלָּא כִּשְׁאָר אֹכֶל בְּעַלְמָא הוּא:


מטמאה טומאת אוכלין (if he gave thought to it – for use as food) – of it came in contact with defilement, that his thought considers it as food. But not the uncleanness of carrion, for it is not meat but rather, merely like the rest of food in general.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:7)


**אֲסוּרָה בַאֲכִילָה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא נָפַק אֶלָּא פּוּרְתָּא וּפְשִׁיטָא דְּכָל מִחוּי הַוָּלָד לֹא הָיָה שָׁם, מִכָּל מָקוֹם חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא בְּאוֹתוֹ מִקְצָת יָצָא רֹאשׁ הַוָּלָד וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְיָלוּד:


אסורה באכילה – and even though only a small part of it (i..e, the placenta–after-birth) came out, and it is obvious that all that all of the cleansing of the fetus was not there, we are concerned perhaps that with that particular piece, the head of the offspring came out and that is considered like it is born.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:8)


[הָכִי גָרְסִינַן] סִימַן וָלָד בָּאִשָּׁה, וְסִימַן וָלָד בַּבְּהֵמָה: הַמְבַכֶּרֶת. זֶהוּ פֶּטֶר רֶחֶם שֶׁלָּהּ:


[הכי גרסינן] סימן ולד באשה. וסימן ולד בבהמה: המבכרת – this is her first issue of her womb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:9)


**יַשְׁלִיכֶנָּה לִכְלָבִים.** שֶׁאֵין קְדֻשָּׁה בָּהּ. דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין שִׁלְיָא בְּלֹא וָלָד, הָכָא רֻבָּא לָאו בַּת מְקַדְּשָׁא הִיא, דְּדִלְמָא נְקֵבָה הֲוָת אוֹ נִדְמֶה הֲוָה וְלֹא קַדִּישׁ:


ישליכנה לכלבים – for there is no sanctity in it. For even though there is no placenta–after-birth without a offspring, here, the majority is not sanctified, for perhaps it was a female, or it was an animal suspected of looking like one (i.e., a lamb looking like a kid and vice-versa – or a hybrid), and it is not holy (see Mishnah 1 of this chapter).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:10)


**וּבַמֻּקְדָּשִׁין.** כְּגוֹן בֶּהֱמַת שְׁלָמִים שֶׁהִפִּילָה שִׁלְיָא:


ובמוקדשין – as, for example, the cattle of peace-offering–well-being which expelled the after-birth–placenta.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:11)


**תִּקָּבֵר.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּאֵין שִׁלְיָא בְּלֹא וָלָד, קַדְּשָׁה, דְּבֵין זָכָר וּבֵין נְקֵבָה דְּנָפֵיק מִבֶּהֱמַת הֶקְדֵּשׁ, קַדִּישׁ:


תקבר – for since there is no after-birth–placenta without offspring, it is sanctified, for whether a male or a female that left from the animal that was sanctified to the Temple is holy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:12)


**בְּפָרָשַׁת דְּרָכִים.** מָקוֹם שֶׁהַדְּרָכִים מִתְפָּרְשִׁים לִשְׁנַיִם, וְדֶרֶךְ הַמְנַחֲשִׁים לְקָבְרָהּ שָׁם כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תַפִּיל עוֹד:


בפרשת דרכים – a place where the paths separate into two. And the path of diviners to bury her there in order that she will not abort anymore.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 4:7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/4:7:13)


**דַּרְכֵי הָאֱמֹרִי.** נִחוּשׁ. וּכְתִיב לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה כְמַעֲשֵׂיהֶם:


דרכי האמורי – sorcery, as it is written (Exodus 23:24}: “[you shall not bow down to the gods in worship] or follow their practices.”



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:1)


**אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ.** הָאֵם וְהַבֵּן, אוֹ הָאֵם וְהַבַּת. אֲבָל הָאָב עִם הַבֵּן אוֹ עִם הַבַּת אֵינוֹ אָסוּר, דְּאוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ מַשְׁמַע מִי שֶׁבְּנוֹ כָּרוּךְ אַחֲרָיו, יָצָא זָכָר שֶׁאֵין בְּנוֹ כָּרוּךְ אַחֲרָיו:


אותו ואת בנו – the mother and the male offspring, or the mother and the female offspring, but the father with the male offspring or with the female offspring is not forbidden [to be slaughtered on the same day], for (Leviticus 22:28): “[However, no animal from the herd or from the flock shall be slaughtered] on the same day with its young,” implies whomever’s offspring is clinging, excluding the male (i.e., the father) whose male offspring is not clinging.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:2)


**בֵּין בָּאָרֶץ בֵּין בְּחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ.** אַיְדֵי דְבָעֵי לְמִתְנֵי בֵּין בְּחֻלִּין בֵּין בְּמֻקְדָּשִׁים, תָּנָא נַמִּי בָּאָרֶץ וּבְחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ, אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא אִצְטְרִיךְ, דְּחוֹבַת הַגּוּף הִיא וְחוֹבַת הַגּוּף נוֹהֵג בֵּין בָּאָרֶץ בֵּין בְּחוּץ לָאָרֶץ:


בין בארץ בין בחוצה לארץ –since it is necessary to teach, whether with unconsecrated things whether with consecrated things, it (i.e., the Mishnah) teaches also “whether in the Land of Israel” or “whether outside the Land of Israel,” even though it is not necessary, for it is the obligation of personal duty (as opposed to laws connected with the soil of the Land of Israel), for personal duty applies whether in the Land of Israel or whether outside the Land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:3)


**בִּפְנֵי הַבַּיִת.** בִּזְמַן שֶׁבֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ קַיָּם:


בפני הבית – at the time when the Temple exists.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:4)


**וְשֶׁלֹּא בִפְנֵי הַבַּיִת.** דְּסָלְקָא דַעְתָּךְ אֲמֵינָא הוֹאִיל וּבְעִנְיָנָא דְּקָדָשִׁים כְּתִיב, בִּפְנֵי הַבַּיִת נִנְהוֹג שֶׁלֹּא בִפְנֵי הַבַּיִת לֹא נִנְהוֹג, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן:


ושלא בפני הבית – you might think I would say, for since it is written regarding the matter of Holy Things, that when the Temple exists, we will apply it, when the Temple doesn’t exist, we will not apply it. Therefore, it comes to tell us the opposite.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:5)


**בְּחֻלִּין וּבְמֻקְדָּשִׁים.** בֵּין שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם חֻלִּין אוֹ שְׁנֵיהֶם מֻקְדָּשִׁים, בֵּין שֶׁאֶחָד מֵהֶן חֻלִּין וְהַשֵּׁנִי מֻקְדָּשִׁים. וּמְנָלָן דְּנוֹהֵג בְּמֻקְדָּשִׁים, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) שׁוֹר אוֹ כֶבֶשׂ אוֹ עֵז כִּי יִוָּלֵד וְגוֹ'‏ יֵרָצֶה לְקָרְבַּן אִשֶּׁה, וּכְתִיב בַּתְרֵיהּ וְשׁוֹר אוֹ שֶׂה אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ וְגוֹ'‏:


בחולין ובמוקדשים – whether both (i.e., the mother and the offspring) are unconsecrated or both are sanctified, and from where in the Torah do we learn that it applies to that which is sanctified? As it is written (Leviticus 22:27): “When an ox or a sheep or a goat is born,[it shall stay seven days with its mother, and from the eight day on] it shall be acceptable as a gift to the LORD,” and afterwards it is written (Leviticus 22:28): “However no animal from the herd or from the flock shall be slaughtered on the same day with its young.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:6)


**בַּחוּץ.** חוּץ לָעֲזָרָה:


בחוץ – outside of the Temple courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:7)


**שְׁנֵיהֶם כְּשֵׁרִים.** מִשּׁוּם דְּבָעֵי לְמִתְנֵי סֵיפָא שְׁנֵיהֶם פְּסוּלִים תָּנָא רֵישָׁא שְׁנֵיהֶם כְּשֵׁרִים:


שניהם כשרים – because it was necessary to teach in the concluding clause [of the Mishnah] that both of them are disqualified, the first clause [of the Mishnah] teaches that both are fit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:8)


**וְהַשֵּׁנִי סוֹפֵג אֶת הָאַרְבָּעִים.** מִשּׁוּם לָאו דְּאוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ. וְלֹא שְׁנָא שָׁחַט אֶת הָאֵם תְּחִלָּה, וְלֹא שְׁנָא שָׁחַט אֶת הַבֵּן תְּחִלָּה. לֹא שְׁנָא שְׁחָטִינְהוּ חַד גַּבְרָא, וְלֹא שְׁנָא תְּרֵי גַבְרֵי:


והשני סופג את הארבעים – because of the negative commandment of “no animal…[shall be slaughtered on the same day] with its young” (Leviticus 22:28). And there is no difference whether he slaughtered the mother first, and makes no difference whether he slaughtered the offspring first. It makes no difference whether both were slaughtered by one person, and makes no difference if two people [slaughtered the mother and its young].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:9)


**הָרִאשׁוֹן חַיָּב כָּרֵת.** מִשּׁוּם שְׁחוּטֵי חוּץ. אֲבָל הַשֵּׁנִי פָּטוּר מִן הַכָּרֵת דְּכֵיוָן דְּנִשְׁחֲטָה אִמּוֹ שׁוּב אֵינוֹ רָאוּי הַבֵּן לִשָּׁחֵט הַיּוֹם בִּפְנִים, דְּפָסוּל מִשּׁוּם מְחֻסַּר זְמַן, שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לְשָׁחֲטוֹ וּלְהַקְרִיבוֹ הַיּוֹם, וְאֵין חַיָּב מִשּׁוּם שׁוֹחֵט בַּחוּץ אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן רָאוּי לִפְנִים, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יז) וְאֶל פֶּתַח אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד לֹא הֱבִיאוֹ, הָרָאוּי לְפֶתַח אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד חַיָּבִין עָלָיו בַּחוּץ, וְאִי לֹא לֹא:


הראשון חייב כרת – because of slaughtering outside of the Temple. But the second [of the pair of the mother and its offspring] is exempt from extirpation, for since the mother was slaughtered, the offspring is further not worthy to be slaughtered today inside, for it is disqualified because of lacking time (i.e., an offering cannot be offered because its time to be offered has not yet arrived), for it is not appropriate to slaughter it and to offer it today, and he is not liable because of slaughtering outside [the Temple] unless it is appropriate inside, as it is written (Leviticus 17:4): “and does not bring it to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting,” that which is appropriate [to be brought] to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, he is liable for it outside, and if not, then not.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:10)


**וּשְׁנֵיהֶם סוֹפְגִים אֶת הָאַרְבָּעִים.** הָרִאשׁוֹן מִשּׁוּם שׁוֹחֵט בַּחוּץ, דְּכָל חַיָּבֵי כְרֵתוֹת לוֹקִין. וְהַשֵּׁנִי מִשּׁוּם לָאו דְּאוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ:


ושניהם סופגים את הארבעים – the first because of slaughtering outside, for all who are liable for violating laws of extirpation are flogged, and the second because of the negative commandment of “the mother and its young.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:11)


**חֻלִּין בִּפְנִים שְׁנֵיהֶם פְּסוּלִין.** מִשּׁוּם חֻלִּין שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטוּ בָעֲזָרָה:


חולין בפנים שנהים פסולין – because unconsecrated offerings which were slaughtered in the Temple courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:12)


**וְהַשֵּׁנִי סוֹפֵג.** מִשּׁוּם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ. אֲבָל מִשּׁוּם חֻלִּין בָּעֲזָרָה, אַזְהָרַת עֲשֵׂה הִיא (דברים יב) כִּי יִרְחַק מִמְּךָ הַמָּקוֹם וְזָבַחְתָּ, בְּרִחוּק מָקוֹם אַתָּה זוֹבֵחַ וְאִי אַתָּה זוֹבֵחַ בְּקֵרוּב מָקוֹם:


והשני סופג – because of “the mother and its young.” But because of unconsecrated animals in the Temple courtyard, there is the warning of a positive commandment (Deuteronomy 12:21): “If the place [where there LORD has chosen to establish His name] is too far from you, you may slaughter [any of the cattle or sheep that the LORD gives you],” when there is a distance of place, you slaughter, but you do not slaughter when the place is close.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:13)


**קָדָשִׁים בִּפְנִים כוּ'‏ הַשֵּׁנִי סוֹפֵג.** מִשּׁוּם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ:


קדשים בפנים כו' והשני סופג – because of “the mother and its young.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:1:14)


**וּפָסוּל.** מִשּׁוּם מְחֻסַּר זְמַן:


ופסול – because of lacking time.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:1)


**חֻלִּין וְקָדָשִׁים בַּחוּץ.** דַּוְקָא נָקַט הָרִאשׁוֹן חֻלִּין וְהַשֵּׁנִי קָדָשִׁים. וְכֵן כָּל הַשְּׁנוּיִין בַּמִּשְׁנָה דַּוְקָא נָקַט לְהוּ:


חולין וקדשים בחוץ – specifically it [i.e., the Mishnah] took the first as unconsecrated produce and the second as Holy Things (i.e., belonging to the Temple). And similarly, all that is taught in the Mishnah, specifically it took them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:2)


**וְהַשֵּׁנִי סוֹפֵג.** מִשּׁוּם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ:


והשני סופג – because of (Leviticus 22:28): “an animal…with its young.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:3)


**קָדָשִׁים.** בַּתְּחִלָּה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ חֻלִּין בַּחוּץ:


קדשים – at first, and afterwards, unconsecrated produce outside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:4)


**הַשֵּׁנִי כָשֵׁר.** בַּאֲכִילָה. וְאַיְדֵי דְתָנָא פָּסוּל, תָּנָא כָּשֵׁר:


והשני כשר – for consumption. But since it (i.e., the Mishnah) taught it was invalid, it taught, it was fit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:5)


**וּשְׁנֵיהֶם סוֹפְגִים.** רִאשׁוֹן מִשּׁוּם שׁוֹחֵט חוּץ, וְשֵׁנִי מִשּׁוּם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ:


ושניהם סופגים – the first because of slaughtering outside [the Temple courtyard], and the second because of “an animal with its young” (Leviticus 22:28)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:6)


**חֻלִּין וְקָדָשִׁים בִּפְנִים שְׁנֵיהֶם פְּסוּלִים.** רִאשׁוֹן מִשּׁוּם חֻלִּין שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטוּ בָעֲזָרָה, וְשֵׁנִי מִשּׁוּם מְחֻסַּר זְמַן:


חולין וקדשים בפנים שניהם פסולים – the first because of unconsecrated animals that were slaughtered in the Temple courtyard, and the second because of lacking time (i.e., that the time to be offered has not yet arrived).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:7)


**וְהַשֵּׁנִי סוֹפֵג.** מִשּׁוּם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ:


והשני סופג – because of “an animal with its young.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:8)


**חֻלִּין בַּחוּץ וּבִפְנִים.** הָרִאשׁוֹן בַּחוּץ וְהַשֵּׁנִי בִּפְנִים:


חולין בחוץ ובפנים – the first slaughtered outside [the Temple courtyard] and the second within [the Temple courtyard].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:9)


**קָדָשִׁים בַּחוּץ וּבִפְנִים הָרִאשׁוֹן בְּכָרֵת.** מִשּׁוּם שׁוֹחֵט בַּחוּץ:


קדשים בחוץ ובפנים הראשון בכרת – because of slaughtering outside[the Temple courtyard].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:10)


**וּשְׁנֵיהֶם פְּסוּלִים.** רִאשׁוֹן מִשּׁוּם שֶׁנִּשְׁחַט בַּחוּץ, וְשֵׁנִי מִשּׁוּם מְחֻסַּר זְמַן:


ושניהם פסולים – the first because it was slaughtered outside [the Temple courtyard] and the second because of lacking time (i.e., that the time to be offered has not yet arrived).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:11)


**וּשְׁנֵיהֶם סוֹפְגִים.** רִאשׁוֹן מִשּׁוּם שׁוֹחֵט חוּץ, וְשֵׁנִי מִשּׁוּם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ:


ושניהם סופגים – the first because of slaughtering outside [the Temple courtyard], and the second because of “an animal with its young.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:2:12)


**קָדָשִׁים בִּפְנִים וּבַחוּץ.** הַשֵּׁנִי סוֹפֵג מִשּׁוּם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ. וּמִשּׁוּם שׁוֹחֵט חוּץ לֹא לָקֵי, דִּמְחֻסַּר זְמַן הוּא וְאֵינוֹ מִתְקַבֵּל בִּפְנִים:


קדשים בפנים ובחוץ – for the second, one receives [forty – actually, thirty nine] lashes] because of “an animal with its young,” but because of slaughtering outside [the Temple courtyard], one is not flogged, for because of lacking time (i.e., that the time to be offered has not yet arrived, and he it is not accepted inside [the Temple courtyard].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:1)


**פָּרַת חַטָּאת.** פָּרָה אֲדֻמָּה. דְּלָאו לַאֲכִילָה הִיא:


פרה חטאת – the red heifer, which is not for consumption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:2)


**וְשׁוֹר הַנִּסְקָל.** לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּגְמַר דִּינוֹ. דְּקַיְמָא לָן דְּאָסוּר בַּהֲנָאָה אֲפִלּוּ שְׁחָטוֹ:


ושור הנסקל – after its verdict has been announced, for we hold hat it is prohibited to derive benefit even when slaughtering it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:3)


**וְעֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ דְּנֶאֱסֶרֶת מֵחַיִּים, וַאֲפִלּוּ שְׁחָטָהּ אֲסוּרָה. וּבַגְּמָרָא מַסִּיק דְּפָרַת חַטָּאת וְעֶגְלָה עֲרוּפָה אֵינָהּ מִשְׁנָה, דִּשְׁנֵיהֶן הַוְיָא שְׁחִיטָתָן שְׁחִיטָה רְאוּיָה, וְצָרִיךְ לַהֲסִירָן מִן הַמִּשְׁנָה, דְּלֹא פָטַר בְּהוּ רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ועגלה ערופה – for he holds that it is forbidden while alive, and even if e slaughtered it, it is forbidden. But in the Gemara (Tractate Hullin 82a) reaches the definitive conclusion that the red heifer and the heifer whose neck is to be broken are not taught in our Mishnah. For regarding both of them, their slaughter is appropriate, and one must remove them from the Mishnah and Rabbi Shimon did not exempt them (for slaughter that is not appropriate for eating is not called slaughter).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְחַיְּבִין.** הַאי דִמְחַיְּבֵי חֲכָמִים בְּשׁוֹחֵט לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, לֹא שָׁנוּ אֶלָּא שֶׁשָּׁחַט רִאשׁוֹן לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה וּבָא שֵׁנִי וְשָׁחַט לְשֻׁלְחָנוֹ לֶאֱכֹל, אֲבָל רִאשׁוֹן לְשֻׁלְחָנוֹ וְשֵׁנִי לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, דִּבְהַךְ שְׁחִיטָה בַּתְרַיְתָּא דְּמִיחַיַּב עֲלָהּ מִשּׁוּם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ אָתֵי נַמִּי דִּין קְטָלָא עֲלוֹהִי, פָּטוּר מִמַּלְקוֹת, דְּקָם לֵיהּ בִּדְרַבָּה מִנֵּיהּ, דְּתַרְתֵּי לֹא עָבְדִינַן בֵּיהּ. וּפְעָמִים שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ שָׁחַט רִאשׁוֹן לְשֻׁלְחָנוֹ וְשֵׁנִי לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה חַיָּב, כְּגוֹן דְּאַתְּרוּ בֵיהּ מִשּׁוּם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ וְלֹא אַתְּרוּ בֵיהּ מִשּׁוּם עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, דְּכֵיוָן דְּלֹא אַתְּרוּ בֵיהּ מִשּׁוּם עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה לֹא מִקְטָל, וְלוֹקֶה מִשּׁוּם אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מחייבין – this that the Sages make him liable when slaughtering for idolatrous purposes, they did not teach other than when he slaughtered the first for idolatrous purposes and the second [animal] came and he slaughtered it for his table to eat it, but if the first was for his table and the second was for idolatrous purposes, that through the latter slaughtering is what makes him liable because of “an animal and its young” (Leviticus 22:28), the law of killing comes upon him, and he is exempt from lashes–flogging because he who has committed two offenses simultaneously, must be held answerable for the severer penalty which is death (i.e., for slaughtering for idolatrous purposes, but he is exempt from flogging for the slaughtering of the animal and her young in one day – see Talmud Hullin 81b) for two [punishments] we don’t do to him. But sometimes, that even when he slaughtered the first for his table and the second for idolatrous purposes, he is liable, as, for example, when they (i.e., witnesses) warned him because of “an animal and its young” (Leviticus 22:28), but they didn’t warn him because of idolatrous purposes, for since they did not warn him regarding idolatrous purposes, he is not killed, but he is flogged because of “an animal and its young.” But the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:5)


**וְנִתְנַבְּלָה בְיָדוֹ.** שֶׁלֹּא מִדַּעַת:


ונתנבלה בידו – inadvertently–unknowingly.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:6)


**וְהַנּוֹחֵר.** שֶׁתּוֹחֵב הַסַּכִּין בִּנְחִירָיו וְחוֹתֵךְ:


והנוחר – inserting the knife in its nostrils and cuts [the windpipe].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:7)


**וְהַמְעַקֵּר.** שֶׁעוֹקֵר הַסִּימָנִים מִמְּקוֹם חִבּוּרָן וְאֵינוֹ שׁוֹחֲטָן:


והמעקר – tearing loose the windpipe and gullet from the place where they are attached and he doesn’t slaughter them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:8)


**פָּטוּר.** וַאֲפִלּוּ לְרַבָּנָן. וְלֹא דַמְיָא לַשְּׁחִיטָה דִלְעֵיל, דְּהָתָם שְׁחִיטָה מְעַלְּיָא אִיכָּא וְדָבָר אַחֵר גּוֹרֵם לָהּ לִפָּסֵל, אֲבָל הָכָא לֵיכָּא שְׁחִיטָה כְּלָל:


פטור – and even according to the Rabbis. But it is not similar to ritual slaughter of above, for there, it is an appropriate ritual slaughter, and another things causes it to be invalidated, but here, there isn’t ritual slaughter at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:9)


**אֵיזֶה שֶׁלָּקַח רִאשׁוֹן יִשְׁחֹט רִאשׁוֹן.** אִם בָּאוּ לְבֵית דִּין, שֶׁבָּא הָאֶחָד לִשְׁחֹט וַחֲבֵרוֹ מְעַכֵּב עָלָיו וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי צָרִיךְ יוֹתֵר מִמְּךָ, אָנוּ אוֹמְרִים לָהֶם הַלּוֹקֵחַ רִאשׁוֹן יִשְׁחַט, שֶׁעַל מְנָת כֵּן לָקַח, שֶׁאִלּוּ לֹא מְכָרָהּ בַּעַל הַבַּיִת לַשֵּׁנִי וְעִכְּבָהּ לְעַצְמוֹ הָיָה הַלּוֹקֵחַ שׁוֹחֵט. שֶׁכָּךְ שָׁנִינוּ בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא, הַלּוֹקֵחַ מִבַּעַל הַבַּיִת הוּא קוֹדֵם לְבַעַל הַבַּיִת, שֶׁעַל מְנָת כֵּן לָקַח:


איזה שלקח ראשון ישחוט ראשון – if they came to the Jewish court, he who came first to slaughter and his fellow detains him and says: “I need it more than you,” we say to them, that the first purchaser should סperson and he detained for himself, the [original] purchaser would slaughter it. For such we taught in the Tosefta (Hullin, Chapter 5, Halakha 5), that he who purposes it from the owner, he precedes the owner, for on that account, he purchased it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:10)


**וְאִם קָדַם הַשֵּׁנִי זָכָה.** שֶׁהִקְדִּים בְּעַצְמוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יָבֹא לִידֵי אִסּוּר, וְיֵשׁ לוֹ רֶוַח שֶׁאוֹכֵל הַיּוֹם בָּשָׂר:


ואם קדם השני זכה – that he advanced himself in order that he not come to a prohibition, and he has the interval that he eats meat today.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:11)


**סוֹפֵג שְׁמוֹנִים.** דְּעַל כָּל בֵּן שֶׁשּׁוֹחֵט עוֹבֵר בְּלָאו:


סופג שמונים – for on every male offspring that he slaughters, he transgresses a negative commandment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:12)


**סוֹפֵג אֶת הָאַרְבָּעִים.** דְּאֵין כָּאן שְׁחִיטַת אִסּוּר אֶלָּא אַחַת:


סופג את ארבעים – for there isn’t here a forbidden slaughtering other than one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:13)


**שְׁחָטָהּ וְאֶת בִּתָּהּ וְאַחַר כָּךְ אֶת בַּת בִּתָּהּ.** יֵשׁ כָּאן שְׁנֵי אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ:


שחטה ואת בתה ואח"כ את בת בתה – there are two [violations] of “an animal and its young.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:14)


**שְׁחָטָהּ וְאֶת בַּת בִּתָּהּ.** אֵין כָּאן עֲדַיִן אִסּוּר. וְאַחַר כָּךְ שָׁחַט אֶת בִּתָּהּ. וְיֵשׁ בִּשְׁחִיטָה זוֹ שְׁנֵי אִסּוּרִים, אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ מִשּׁוּם אִמָּהּ, וּבְנוֹ וְאוֹתוֹ מִשּׁוּם בִּתָּהּ שֶׁל זוֹ שֶׁכְּבָר נִשְׁחֲטָה:


שחטה ואת בת בתה – there is [not] yet here a prohibition. ואח"כ שחט את בתה – and there is in this slaughtering two prohibitions, “an animal and its young” because of its mother,” and an offspring and it because of its female offspring of that which has already been slaughtered.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:15)


**סוֹפֵג אַרְבָּעִים.** דְּחַד לָאו הוּא וַחֲדָא הַתְרָאָה וְחַד מַעֲשֶׂה:


סופג ארבעים – it is one negative commandment ‘violated] and one warning and one deed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:16)


**סוּמְכוֹס אוֹמֵר סוֹפֵג שְׁמוֹנִים.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְסוּמְכוֹס דְּמִחַיַּב בְּהַתְרָאָה אַחַת וּבְלָאו אֶחָד שְׁתֵּי מַלְקֻיּוֹת. וְהוּא הַדִּין לְרֵישָׁא דְּקָתָנֵי שָׁחַט שְׁנֵי בָנֶיהָ וְאַחַר כָּךְ שְׁחָטָהּ סוֹפֵג אַרְבָּעִים, לְסוּמְכוֹס סוֹפֵג שְׁמוֹנִים. וְכָךְ הִיא בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא, שָׁחַט חֲמִשָּׁה בָנֶיהָ וְאַחַר כָּךְ שְׁחָטָהּ, סוּמְכוֹס אוֹמֵר מִשּׁוּם רַבִּי מֵאִיר חַיָּב מִשּׁוּם חֲמִשָּׁה לָאוִין:


סומכוס אומר סופג שמונים – for Sumachos holds that he is liable for one warning and with one negative commandment, two [punishments of] stripes. And the same law applies with the first clause [of the Mishnah] which teaches that if he slaughtered its two offspring and afterwards slaughtered her (i.e., the mother), he receives forty (actually thirty-nine) lashes. According to Sumachos, he receives eighty lashes. Such it is in the Tosefta (Hullin, Chapter 5, Halakha 7), if he slaughters her five offspring and afterwards slaughter it (i.e., the mother), Sumachos states in the name of Rabbi Meir that he is liable because of [the violation] of five negative commandments.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:17)


**בְּאַרְבָּעָה פְרָקִים בַּשָּׁנָה.** דֶּרֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לַעֲשׂוֹת סְעֻדּוֹת בְּאַרְבָּעָה פְרָקִים הַלָּלוּ, וּסְתָם הַלּוֹקֵחַ בְּהֵמָה אֵינוֹ לוֹקֵחַ אֶלָּא לִשְׁחֹט מִיָּד, לְפִיכָךְ הַמּוֹכֵר בְּהֵמָה לַחֲבֵרוֹ וּמָכַר תְּחִלָּה אִמָּהּ אוֹ בִתָּהּ בּוֹ בַּיּוֹם, צָרִיךְ שֶׁיֹּאמַר לַשֵּׁנִי דַּע לְךָ שֶׁהַיּוֹם מָכַרְתִּי אִמָּהּ לִשְׁחֹט אוֹ בִתָּהּ מָכַרְתִּי לִשְׁחֹט, שֶׁמָּא כְּבָר נִשְׁחֲטָה:


בארבעה פרים בשנה – it is the manner of Israel to make meals on these four seasons [of the year], for a person who simply purchases an animal does not purchase it other than to slaughter it immediately, therefore, a person who sells an animal to his fellow and he sells first its mother or its female offspring on that selfsame day, he must state to the second [person]: “Know for yourself that I sold its mother for slaughter” or “I sold its female offspring for slaughter” lest it was already slaughtered.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:18)


**בְּיוֹם טוֹב הָאַחֲרוֹן שֶׁל חָג.** הָיוּ מַרְבִּים בְּשִׂמְחָה מִפְּנֵי שֶׁרֶגֶל בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ הוּא וְחָבִיב עֲלֵיהֶן. וְהַאי דְּלָא חֲשִׁיב עֶרֶב יוֹם טוֹב רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁל חָג, מִשּׁוּם דְּכֻלֵּי עַלְמָא טְרִידֵי בְּסֻכָּה וְלוּלָב וְאֵין לָהֶם פְּנַאי לְהַרְבּוֹת בִּשְׁחִיטָה:


ביום טוב האחרון של חג – they would increase in joy because it is a festival of its own and was beloved to them. And this did not consider the eve of the first day of the Festival, because the entire world is preoccupied with [the building of] the Sukkah and [acquiring] a Lulav [and Etrog], and they don’t have free time to make a large slaughtering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:19)


**אַף עֶרֶב יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים בַּגָּלִיל.** וְלֹא בִיהוּדָה וּבִשְׁאָר אֲרָצוֹת, שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ אוֹכְלִים עֶרֶב יוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים אֶלָּא בְּשַׂר עוֹף וְדָגִים כִּדְמוּכָח בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה גַּבֵּי הַהוּא חַיָּטָא דִּזְבַן נוּנָא:


אף ערבי יום הכיפורים בגלילי – but not in Judea and not in the rest of the lands, for they would not eat on the Eve of Yom Kippur anything other than meat, children and–or fish as is proven in Bereshit Rabbah, regarding a particular tailor who sold a fish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:20)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין לוֹ רֶוַח.** הֶפְסֵק בֵּינְתַּיִם, שֶׁמָּכַר הָאֵם הַיּוֹם:


בזמן שאין לו ריוח – interruption between them, that he sold the mother [animal] today.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:21)


**אֲבָל יֶשׁ לוֹ רֶוַח.** שֶׁמָּכַר אֶת הָרִאשׁוֹנָה אֶתְמוֹל וְהַשְּׁנִיָּה הַיּוֹם:


אבל יש לו ריוח – that he sold the first yesterday and the second today.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:22)


**אֵין צָרִיךְ לְהוֹדִיעוֹ.** שֶׁאֲנִי אוֹמֵר אֶתְמוֹל שְׁחָטָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין לוֹ רֶוַח שֶׁהוּא נֶחְפָּז וּמָהִיר לִקְנוֹת שֶׁמַּרְאֶה שֶׁרוֹצֶה לִשְׁחֹט הַיּוֹם. אֲבָל יֶשׁ לוֹ רֶוַח שֶׁאֵינוֹ נֶחְפָּז בִּקְנִיָּתוֹ, אֵינוֹ חַיָּב לְהוֹדִיעוֹ, דְּשֶׁמָּא לְצֹרֶךְ יוֹם אַחֵר הוּא קוֹנֶה. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה לְפֵרוּשֵׁי מִלְּתֵיהּ דְּתַנָּא קַמָּא אָתֵי וְלֹא לְאִפְלוֹגֵי עֲלֵיהּ:


אין צריך להודיעו – for I say: “Yesterday, I slaughtered it first.” Another explanation: At the time when he doesn’t have space of time that he is in haste and hurries to purchase that shows that he wants to slaughter today. But if he has a space of time that he is not in haste to purchase it, he is not required to inform him, for lest it was for the need of another day that he purchases it. But Rabbi Yehuda, he comes to explain the matter of the first Tanna–teacher and not to dispute it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:23)


**וּמוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוּדָה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלָּקַח זֶה הַיּוֹם וְזֶה לְמָחָר. וְלַפֵּרוּשׁ הַשֵּׁנִי, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ נֶחְפָּז וּמָהִיר לִקְנוֹת:


ומודה ר' יהודה – even hough that he purchased this [animal] today and that [animal] on the morrow, And regarding the second explanation, even though he is not in haste and hurried to purchase.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:3:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:3:24)


**אֶת הָאֵם לֶחָתָן וְאֶת הַבַּת לַכַּלָּה.** אוֹרְחָא דְמִלְּתָא נָקַט, דְּאוֹרַח אַרְעָא לַעֲשׂוֹת סְעֻדָּה טְפֵי בְּבֵית הֶחָתָן מִבְּבֵית הַכַּלָּה, הִלְכָּךְ הָאֵם לֶחָתָן שֶׁהִיא גְדוֹלָה, וְהַבַּת שֶׁהִיא קְטַנָּה לַכַּלָּה:


את האם לחת ואת הבת לכלה – it is a usual incident, for it is the custom of the world to make a large meal at the house of the groom from the house of the bride; therefore, the mother fo the grow who is larger, and the offspring which is smaller to the bride.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:4:1)


**מַשְׁחִיטִין אֶת הַטַּבָּח בְּעַל כָּרְחוֹ.** שֶׁאִם קִבֵּל דִּינָר מִלּוֹקֵחַ לִתֵּן לוֹ בְּדִינָר בָּשָׂר:


He slaughters the meat against his will: That if he accepts a Dinar from the buyer, he gives him a Dinar's worth of meat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:4:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ שׁוֹר שָׁוֶה אֶלֶף.** זוּז, שׁוֹחֲטוֹ בְּעַל כָּרְחוֹ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ לוֹקְחִים לִשְׁאָר הַבָּשָׂר. לְפִי שֶׁדְּבַר תּוֹרָה מָעוֹת קוֹנוֹת וּמֵעֵת שֶׁקִּבֵּל הַמּוֹכֵר הַדִּינָר קָנָה הַלּוֹקֵחַ הַבָּשָׂר. וְלֹא תִקְּנוּ חֲכָמִים שֶׁמְּשִׁיכָה קוֹנָה וְלֹא מָעוֹת אֶלָּא גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יֹאמַר לוֹ מוֹכֵר לַקּוֹנֶה נִשְׂרְפוּ חִטֶּיךָ בָּעֲלִיָּה. דְּאִי מוֹקְמָת לְהוּ לְפֵירֵי בְּאַחְרַיוּת הַלּוֹקֵחַ מִשֶּׁנָּתַן הַמָּעוֹת, אִי מִתְרְמֵי דְּלֵקָה בְּבֵית הַמּוֹכֵר שֶׁהַפֵּרוֹת שָׁם לֹא טָרַח לְאַצוֹלִינְהוּ. אֲבָל לְאַחַר שֶׁמָּשַׁךְ מִסְּתָמָא מַמְטֵי לְהוּ לְבֵיתֵיהּ. וּבְאַרְבָּעָה פְרָקִים אֵלּוּ הֶעֱמִידוּ חֲכָמִים דִּבְרֵיהֶם עַל דִּין תּוֹרָה שֶׁהַמָּעוֹת קוֹנוֹת:


..


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:4:3)


**לְפִיכָךְ אִם מֵת מֵת לַלּוֹקֵחַ.** וּמַפְסִיד לַדִּינָר, שֶׁהֲרֵי בִּרְשׁוּתוֹ מֵת:


לפיכך אם מת מת ללוקח – And he loses his Denar, for it died when it was in his domain–possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:4:4)


**אֵינוֹ כֵן.** דְּבָעִינַן מְשִׁיכָה וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא מָשַׁךְ חוֹזֵר בּוֹ הַטַּבָּח:


אינו כן – for we require “pulling” and all the while that he didn’t “pull,” the butcher can retract.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/5:5:1)


**יוֹם אֶחָד הָאָמוּר בְּאוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ כוּ'.** לְפִי שֶׁפָּרָשַׁת אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ סְמוּכָה לְקָדָשִׁים, דִּכְתִיב יֵרָצֶה לְקָרְבַּן אִשֶּׁה, וְסָמִיךְ לֵיהּ אוֹתוֹ וְאֶת בְּנוֹ. וּבְקָדָשִׁים לַיְלָה הוֹלֵךְ אַחַר הַיּוֹם, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא ז) בְּיוֹם קָרְבָּנוֹ יֵאָכֵל לֹא יַנִּיחַ מִמֶּנּוּ עַד בֹּקֶר, אַלְמָא לַיְלָה שֶׁלְּאַחֲרָיו קָרוּי יוֹם קָרְבָּנוֹ עַד הַבֹּקֶר. יָכוֹל אַף זֶה כֵּן [נֶאֱמַר כָּאן יוֹם אֶחָד] וְנֶאֱמַר בְּמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית יוֹם אֶחָד וְכוּ':


יום אחד האמור באותו ואת בנו כו' – because the portion of “the animal and its young” (Leviticus 22:28) is adjacent to the sacrifice, and it is written (Leviticus 22:27): “[ad from the eighth day on] it shall be acceptable as a gift to [the LORD],” and adjacent to it, “the animal and its young” (Leviticus 22:28). But with Holy Things, the night follows after the day, as it is written (Leviticus 7:15): “[And the flesh of his thanksgiving sacrifice of well-being] shall be eaten on the day that it is offered; none of it shall be set aside until morning.” So we see that the night that is after it is called the “on the day...until morning.” It is possible that even this is the case [it is stated here, “one day”] and it is stated in the Creation story (Genesis 1:5): “[And there was evening and there was morning] the first day.”



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/6:1:1)
