[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:1)


**בַּטְּהוֹרָה וּבַטְּמֵאָה.** בֵּין שֶׁהִיא טְהוֹרָה שְׁחוּטָה וְהַמַּפְשִׁיט טָמֵא, בֵּין שֶׁהִיא נְבֵלָה טְמֵאָה וְהַמַּפְשִׁיט טָהוֹר, אִם מַפְשִׁיטוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת מִן הָעוֹר שָׁטִיחַ דְּהַיְנוּ מַצָּע לְהַצִּיעַ עַל גַּבֵּי הַמִּטָּה אוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי שֻׁלְחָן, כְּגוֹן שֶׁקּוֹרֵעַ וְחוֹתֵךְ הָעוֹר כֻּלּוֹ לְאָרְכּוֹ מֵרֹאשׁ הַבְּהֵמָה וְעַד זְנָבָהּ, וּמַתְחִיל לְהַפְשִׁיט הָעוֹר, הֲוֵי חִבּוּר יָד לְהוֹצִיא טֻמְאָה מִן הַנְּבֵלָה אִם נוֹגֵעַ אָדָם טָהוֹר בָּעוֹר, וּלְהַכְנִיס טֻמְאָה לַבָּשָׂר אִם טְהוֹרָה הִיא:


בטהורה ובטמאה – whether it (i.e., the animal – beast or wild animal) is ritually slaughtered as a pure animal and the one who flays it is impure, or whether it is a impure carrion and the one who flays it is pure, if he flays it to make from the hide a spread–mat, that is, a mattress to spread out on top of the bed or on top of a table, as for example, when he tears and severs the hide completely lengthwise from the head of the animal until its tail, and he begins to flay the hide, there would be a connecting handle to remove the defilement from the carrion if a pure individual comes in contact with the hide, to bring in the defilement to the meat if it is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:2)


**עַד כְּדֵי אֲחִיזָה.** שֶׁהֵן שְׁנֵי טְפָחִים. וּטְפֵי מֵהָכִי לֹא הֲוֵי יָד לְהַכְנִיס וּלְהוֹצִיא טֻמְאָה, וְאִם הַבָּשָׂר טָהוֹר וְנָגְעָה טֻמְאָה בְּיוֹתֵר מִשְּׁנֵי טְפָחִים אֵלּוּ לֹא נִטְמָא הַבָּשָׂר, וְאִם הַבָּשָׂר נְבֵלָה וְנָגַע טָהוֹר בְּיוֹתֵר מִשְּׁנֵי טְפָחִים הַלָּלוּ לֹא נִטְמָא הַטָּהוֹר:


עד כדי אחיזה (enough for a hold [on the carcass]) – which are two handbreadths, but more than this it is not handle to bring in or to remove defilement, but if the meat is pure and defilement touched it with more than these two handbreadths, the meat was not defiled, but if the meat was carrion and something pure that is more than two handbreadths touched it, these did not defile the pure [meat].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:3)


**וּלְחֵמֶת.** אִם לֹא חָתַךְ הָעוֹר מִתְּחִלָּה לְאָרְכּוֹ אֶלָּא מַפְשִׁיטוֹ כָּפוּל לְצֹרֶךְ חֵמֶת, מַתְחִיל מִצַּוָּאר וְהוֹפְכוֹ כְּלַפֵּי זְנָבָהּ:


ולחמת (waterskin) – if he did not sever the hide lengthwise from the beginning, but rather flays it double for the needs of the waterskin, he begins from the neck and turns it over towards its tail.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:4)


**עַד שֶׁיַּפְשִׁיט אֶת הֶחָזֶה.** הֲוֵי חִבּוּר. וְהַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּעוֹר כְּנוֹגֵעַ בַּבָּשָׂר בֵּין לִטָּמֵא בֵּין לְטַמֵּא. מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֶחָזֶה קָשֶׁה לְהַפְשִׁיט מִכָּל הָאֵבָרִים:


עד שיפשיט את החזה (until he will flay the breast) – which is the connector. And one who touches the hide is like one who touches the meat–skin – whether to become impure–contract uncleanness or whether to make impure–impart it, because the breast is the hardest to flay from all of the limbs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:5)


**הַמַּרְגִּיל.** שֶׁמַּתְחִיל מֵרַגְלֵי הַבְּהֵמָה לְהַפְשִׁיט וּמַפְשִׁיטוֹ כָּפוּל לְצֹרֶךְ חֵמֶת:


המרגיל (one who flays the animal from the foot upwards (the entire skin is considered as connected with the flesh – as long as the process lasts – for purposes of Levitical uncleanness) – that he begins from the feet of the animal to flay and he flays it double for the needs of the waterskin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:6)


**כֻּלּוֹ חִבּוּר.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֶחָזֶה לְסוֹף הֶפְשֵׁטוֹ הוּא, לְפִיכָךְ כֻּלּוֹ חִבּוּר עַד הֶחָזֶה, וְהַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּעוֹר הַמֻּפְשָׁט כְּנוֹגֵעַ בַּבָּשָׂר בֵּין לִטָּמֵא בֵּין לְטַמֵּא:


כולו חבור – because e he breast is the end of his flaying. Therefore, it is all connected until the breast, and a person who touches the flayed hide, l=is like touching the skin–meat whether to impart uncleanness or to become unclean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:7)


**עוֹר שֶׁעַל הַצַּוָּאר.** מֵעַצְמוֹ נִפְשָׁט, לְפִיכָךְ אֵינוֹ חִבּוּר לַעֲשׂוֹת הַמֻּפְשָׁט הָרִאשׁוֹן חִבּוּר כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהֶחָזֶה עוֹשֶׂה:


עור שעל הצואר – and it was flayed of itself. Therefore, it is not connected to do the first playing that is connected in the manner that the breast makes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:3:8)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים וְכוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים וכו' – and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:1)


**עוֹר שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלָיו כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר.** בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד:


עור שיש עליו כזית בשר – in one place [of carrion meat].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:2)


**הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּצִיב הַיּוֹצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ.** רְצוּעָה וְתִלְתּוּל הַיּוֹצֵא מֵאוֹתוֹ בָשָׂר וְתָלוּי וְדָבוּק בְּמִקְצָת. וּבְאוֹתוֹ צִיב לֵיכָּא כַּזַּיִת אֲבָל מְעֻרֶה הוּא לְכַזַּיִת:


הנוגע בציב היוצא ממנו (the tassel–shred that proceeds from it – the tassel-like ends of the hide) – a strip and a wart that goes out from the same flesh and is suspended and connected in part. And with that tassel, there isn’t an olive’s handbreadth, but it is from its hide of an olive’s handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:3)


**וּבְשַׂעֲרָה.** שֶׁבָּעוֹר כְּנֶגֶד אוֹתוֹ בָשָׂר:


ובשערה – that is on the hide opposite that skin–meat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:4)


**טָמֵא.** דְּשַׂעֲרָה הֲוֵי שׁוֹמֵר:


טמא – for the hair is a protection.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:5)


**מְטַמֵּא בְמַשָּׂא.** שֶׁהֲרֵי נָשָׂא כַּזַּיִת נְבֵלָה:


מטמא במשא – for it carries an olive’s bulk of carrion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:6)


**וְלֹא בְמַגָּע.** דְּאִי אֶפְשָׁר לִגַּע בְּיַחַד, וּשְׁתֵּי נְגִיעוֹת אֵינָן מִצְטָרְפוֹת:


ולא במגע – for it is impossible to touch together, and the two touches–contacts do not combine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:7)


**לֹא בְמַגָּע וְלֹא בְמַשָּׂא.** כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ טַעֲמָא בְּסֵיפָא שֶׁהָעוֹר מְבַטְּלָן:


לא במגע ולא במשא – as it explains the reason in the concluding [clause], that the hide renders them negligible.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:4:8)


**וּמוֹדֶה רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא.** דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא הֲוֵי כַּזַּיִת מְעֻרֶה בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד, בִּכְהַאי גַוְנָא כְּגוֹן שֶׁתָּחַב שְׁנֵי חֲצָאֵי זֵיתִים בְּקֵיסָם, כְּלוֹמַר עֵץ דַּק, וֶהֱסִיטָן, שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


ומודה ר"ע – and even though there isn’t an olive’s bulk from its hide in one place, in the same manner as for example, when he inserts two halves of olives on a spindle–chip, meaning to say, on a thin piece of wood, and shook them, that it is ritually impure. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:5:1)


**קוּלִית.** כָּל עֶצֶם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ מֹחַ קָרוּי קוּלִית. וְקוּלִית הַמֵּת הַנּוֹגֵעַ בָּהּ בֵּין סְתוּמָה בֵּין נְקוּבָה טָמֵא, דְּהָא עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה בַּמֵּת מְטַמֵּא בְּמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא, כְּדִכְתִיב (במדבר יט) אוֹ בְעֶצֶם אָדָם:


קולית (marrow-bone) – every bone that has in marrow is called a marrow-bone. And the marrow-bone of a corpse, one who touches it, whether it is stopped up or whether it is hollowed out, is ritually impure, for a bone like a barley corn of a corpse defiles through contact and carrying, as it is written (Numbers 19:16): “[And in the open, anyone who touches a person who was killed or who died naturally,] or human bone [or a grave, shall be impure seven days].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:5:2)


**וְקוּלִית הַמֻּקְדָּשִׁים.** שֶׁהַפִּגּוּל וְהַנּוֹתָר מְטַמְּאִין הַיָּדַיִם. דְּגָזְרוּ בְּהוּ רַבָּנָן מִשּׁוּם חַשְׁדֵי כְהֻנָּה וְעַצְלֵי כְהֻנָּה, וְגָזְרוּ אַף בַּעֲצָמוֹת שֶׁשִּׁמְּשׁוּ אֶת הַנּוֹתָר:


וקולית המוקדשים – the sacrifice that is rejected in consequence of improper intention in the mind of the officiating priest and the portions of the sacrifices left over beyond the legal time and bound to be burnt defile the hands, for the Rabbis decreed regarding them because of suspected Kohanim (of willfully unfitting a sacrifice in order to spite the owner) and–or Kohanim who shirked their duties, and they decreed even with the bones hat were used for the portions of the sacrifices left over beyond the legal time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:5:3)


**קוּלִית נְבֵלָה.** נְבֵלָה אֵין עַצְמוֹתֶיהָ מְטַמְּאִין, דִּכְתִיב בְּנִבְלָתָהּ, וְלֹא בָעֲצָמוֹת. וְכֵן שֶׁרֶץ. הִלְכָּךְ סְתוּמִים טְהוֹרִים אַף מִלְּטַמֵּא בְמַשָּׂא, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן בְּמַגָּע, דְּאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹ לִגַּע בַּמֹּחַ שֶׁבִּפְנִים. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּשׁוֹמֵר מַכְנִיס וּמוֹצִיא טֻמְאָה, הָנֵי מִלֵּי בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר לִגַּע בַּטֻּמְאָה עַצְמָהּ וַאֲפִלּוּ לֹא נָגַע בָּהּ אֶלָּא בַשּׁוֹמֵר, טָמֵא, אֲבָל הֵיכָא דְּאִי אֶפְשָׁר לִגַּע בַּטֻּמְאָה עַצְמָהּ, אֵין שׁוֹמֵר מְטַמֵּא:


קולית נבלה – the marrow-bone of a carrion – its bones do not defile, as it is written (Leviticus 11:39): “[If an animal that you may eat has died, anyone who touches] its carcass [shall be impure until evening],” and not the bones, and similarly for the moving creatures–reptiles. Therefore, those that are stopped up are pure, even from being defiled by carrying, and all the more so, through contact, for it is impossible for it to touch the bone-marrow that is inside. But even though the guard brings in and removes defilement, these words refer to a thing where it is possible to touch the defilement itself, even if only the guard touched it, it is impure, but where it is impossible to touch the defilement itself, the guard does not defile it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:5:4)


**נְקוּבִים כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** אֲפִלּוּ כְּחוּט הַשַּׂעֲרָה שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר לְהַכְנִיס בּוֹ חוּט הַשַּׂעֲרָה שֶׁל רֹאשׁוֹ אוֹ זְקָנוֹ וַהֲרֵי הוּא מִטַּמֵּא בְּכָךְ:


נקובים כל שהוא (hollowed out in any amount whatsoever) – een like the thread of hair where it is possible to bring into it a thread of hair of his head or his beard, for he becomes defiled through thins.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:5:5)


**מִנַּיִן שֶׁאַף בְּמַשָּׂא.** אַקּוּלִית דִּנְבֵלָה קָאֵי, דְּשֶׁרֶץ אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְמַשָּׂא:


מנין שאף במשא – it is referring to the bone-marrow of a carrion, for a moving creature–unclean reptile does not defile through carrying.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:1)


**בֵּיצַת הַשֶּׁרֶץ.** יֵשׁ בִּשְׁמֹנָה שְׁרָצִים שֶׁמְּטִילִים בֵּיצִים, כְּגוֹן הַצָּב וְהַלְּטָאָה וְהַחֹמֶט:


ביצת השרץ – there are eight moving creatures–creeping things that attach eggs, such as the toad, the sand lizard and the lizard (see Leviticus 11:29-30: “The following shall be impure for you among the things that swarm on the earth: the mole, the mouse, and great lizards of every variety; the gecko, the land crocodile, the lizard, the sand lizard, and the chameleon.”).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:2)


**הַמְרֻקֶּמֶת.** שֶׁנּוֹצַר בָּהּ אֶפְרוֹחַ:


המרוקמת (the egg of a creeping thing in a developed state) – where a chick–young bird is formed within.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:3)


**טְהוֹרָה.** לְפִי שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לִגַּע בָּאֶפְרוֹחַ שֶׁבִּפְנִים:


טהורה – because it is impossible to touch the chick–young bird that is inside.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:4)


**נִקְּבָה כָל שֶׁהוּא טְמֵאָה.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָגַע. שֶׁהַשּׁוֹמֵר מַכְנִיס וּמוֹצִיא טֻמְאָה בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר לִגַּע:


ניקבה כל שהוא טמאה (if it is pierced in any measure, it is impure) – and even though he didn’t touch it, for the guard brings it and removes the defilement in something that is possible to touch.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:5)


**עַכְבָּר שֶׁחֶצְיוֹ בָשָׂר.** יֵשׁ מִין עַכְבָּר שֶׁאֵינוֹ פָּרֶה וְרָבֶה אֶלָּא מֵעַצְמוֹ נוֹצָר מִן הָאֲדָמָה, כְּאַשְׁפָּה הַמַּשְׁרֶצֶת תּוֹלָעִים. וְאִם עֲדַיִן לֹא נִשְׁלְמָה בְּרִיאַת הָעַכְבָּר אֶלָּא מִצִּדּוֹ אֶחָד הַיְמָנִי אוֹ הַשְּׂמָאלִי. הַנּוֹגֵעַ בַּבָּשָׂר, טָמֵא. בָּאֲדָמָה שֶׁכְּנֶגְדּוֹ, טָהוֹר:


עכבר שחציו בשר – there is a species of mouse which is not “fruitful and multiply” but rather, from itself, it is formed from the ground, like garbage that breeds worms, and if it still has not completed the creation of the mouse, other than from one side – the right or the left, he who touches the flesh is ritually impure. The earth over against the flesh is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:6:6)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר כו' – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:1)


**הָאֵבָר וְהַבָּשָׂר הַמְדֻלְדָּלִים בִּבְהֵמָה.** נִתְלַשׁ מִמֶּנָּה אֵבָר דְּהַיְנוּ בָּשָׂר וְגִידִים וַעֲצָמוֹת, אוֹ נִתְלַשׁ מִמֶּנָּה בָּשָׂר לְבַדּוֹ, וַעֲדַיִן הֵן מְעֻרִין בָּהּ בְּמִקְצָת. וְיֵשׁ הֶפְרֵשׁ בֵּין אֵבָר מִן הַחַי לְבָשָׂר מִן הַחַי, שֶׁהָאֵבָר מְטַמֵּא אָדָם וְכֵלִים כִּנְבֵלָה, וְהַבָּשָׂר שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֵבָר, טָהוֹר מִכְּלוּם, כִּדְיָלְפִינַן מִקְּרָא דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יא) וְכִי יָמוּת מִן הַבְּהֵמָה, מִמִּקְצַת בְּהֵמָה כְּגוֹן אֵבָר מִמֶּנָּה, וְסֵיפָא דִקְרָא הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּנִבְלָתָהּ יִטְמָא, וּמִדִּכְתִיב וְכִי יָמוּת מַשְׁמַע כְּעֵין מִיתָה בָּעִינַן שֶׁאֵינָהּ עוֹשָׂה חֲלִיפִין, לַאֲפוֹקֵי בָּשָׂר שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה חֲלִיפִין, שֶׁאִם יִתְלֹשׁ בָּשָׂר מִן הַבְּהֵמָה, בָּשָׂר אַחֵר עוֹלֶה תַחְתָּיו:


האבר והבשר המדולדלים בבהמה – the limb is detached from it, which are the flesh, and sinews and bones, or only the flesh is detached from it, but they still are stirred up in it slightly. But there is a difference between a limb from a living animal to flesh from a living animal For the limb defiles man and utensils like a carrion, but the flesh which is not a limb, is pure from nothing, as we derive from Scripture as it is written (Leviticus 11:39): “If an animal that you may eat has died,” from part of he animal, as, for example, a limb from it, but the end of the Biblical verse, “anyone who touches its carcass shall be impure [until evening],” but since it is written, “if [an animal hat you may eat] has died,” implies that we require something similar to death that does not make an exchange–substitution, excluding flesh that makes substitutions that if one tears off flesh from the animal, other flesh will rise up under it (i.e., in its place).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:2)


**מְטַמְּאִין טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִין.** אִם חָשַׁב עֲלֵיהֶן לְהַאֲכִילָן לְנָכְרִי, הֲווּ אֹכֶל לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה וּלְטַמֵּא אֲחֵרִים. דְּטֻמְאַת עַצְמָן אֵין בָּהֶן עַד שֶׁיִּתָּלְשׁוּ כֻלָּן, אֲבָל מְקַבְּלִין טֻמְאָה מִן הַשֶּׁרֶץ וּמְטַמְּאִין בָּהּ אֶת אֲחֵרִים:


מטמאין טומאת אוכלין – if he thought about them to feed them to a heathen, they would be food that received defilement and [are able] to defile others, for their own defilement isn’t within them until he tears out all of them (i.e., the flesh, sinews and bones), but one receives defilement from the creeping thing and through it, they defile the others.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:3)


**וּצְרִיכִים הֶכְשֵׁר.** לָבֹא בַמַּיִם לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּדַּלְדְּלוּ, וְאַחַר כָּךְ מְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה לְעוֹלָם:


וצריכים הכשר – to come into water after they are detached, and afterwards they are susceptible to ritual impurity forever.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:4)


**נִשְׁחֲטָה הַבְּהֵמָה.** נִטְהֲרוּ מִלְּטַמֵּא עוֹד מִשּׁוּם נְבֵלָה. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֲסוּרִים בַּאֲכִילָה מִשּׁוּם וּבָשָׂר בַּשָּׂדֶה טְרֵיפָה, יְדֵי נְבֵלָה טָהֲרוּ, דְּאֵין שְׁחִיטָה עוֹשָׂה נִפּוּל:


נשחטה הבהמה – they were purified from defiling more because of being carrion. But even though they are forbidden for consumption because of (Exodus 22:30): “[you must not eat] flesh torn by beasts in the field,” through carrion they became purified, for ritual slaughter does not make a limb detached from the body (see Leviticus 11:32 – and Talmud Hullin 74a – the natural death of an animal causes the hanging limb to be considered as if detached – in life-time, so that it does not come under the law of carrion – but slaughtering does not – and the dangling limb is considered as a part of the slaughtered animal – see also Talmud Hullin 129a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:5)


**הֻכְשְׁרוּ בְדָמֶיהָ.** לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה בְּלֹא הֶכְשֵׁר אַחֵר, כְּדִין בְּהֵמָה שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטָה וְיָצָא מִמֶּנָּה דָם שֶׁהֻכְשַׁר בְּשָׂרָהּ בְּאוֹתוֹ דָם:


הוכשרו בדמיה – to be able to receive defilement without other preparation, like the law of the beast that was slaughtered and blood came out from it when its flesh had been preparated with that same blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:6)


**מֵתָה הַבְּהֵמָה הַבָּשָׂר.** שֶׁנִּדַּלְדֵּל: **צָרִיךְ הֶכְשֵׁר.** לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה מִן הַשֶּׁרֶץ אִם לֹא הֻכְשַׁר מִשֶּׁנִּדַּלְדֵּל. דְּטֻמְאַת נְבֵלָה אֵין בּוֹ, דְּמִיתָה עוֹשָׂה נִפּוּל וְנֶחְשָׁב כְּאִלּוּ נָפַל אוֹתוֹ הָאֵבָר אוֹ אוֹתוֹ הַבָּשָׂר הַמְדֻלְדָּל וְאֵינוֹ נַעֲשֶׂה נְבֵלָה עִמָּהּ בְּמִיתָתָהּ, אֶלָּא כְּאִלּוּ פֵרֵשׁ מֵחַיִּים הוּא, וּבָשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַחַי טָהוֹר:


מתה הבהמה הבשר – that was dangling. צריך הכשר – to be susceptible for ritual impurity from the creeping animal if it had not been prepared once its skin was dangling. For it lacks the defilement of the carrion, for death makes the falling off of a limb detached from the body and it is considered as if that limb fell or that dangling flesh, and it is not made a carrion with it in its death, but rather, it separated from life, it and the flesh that separates from the pure life.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:7)


**הָאֵבָר מְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם אֵבָר מִן הַחַי.** דְּמִיתָה עוֹשָׂה נִפּוּל כִּדְפָרֵישְׁנָא, וְאֵינוֹ נַעֲשֶׂה נְבֵלָה עִמָּהּ, הִלְכָּךְ אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם נְבֵלָה. וְאִיכָּא בֵּין אֵבָר מִן הַחַי לְאֵבָר מִן הַנְּבֵלָה, דְּבָשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מֵאֵבָר מִן הַחַי לֹא מְטַמֵּא, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֲפִלּוּ בָּשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַחַי לֹא מְטַמֵּא, כָּל שֶׁכֵּן בָּשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מֵאֵבָר מִן הַחַי, וְאִלּוּ בָּשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מֵאֵבָר מִן הַנְּבֵלָה מְטַמֵּא כְּבָשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִן הַנְּבֵלָה עַצְמָהּ:


האבר מטמא משום אבר מן החי – for death makes the hanging limb to be considered as if detached as we have explained, and does not make a carrion with it. Therefore, it does not defile because of carrion. But there is a difference between a limb from a living animal and a limb from a carrion, for flesh that separates from the limb of a living animal does not defile, for even flesh that separates from the living does not defile all the more so, flesh that separates from the limb of a living animal, whereas flesh that separates from a limb from a carrion defiles like the flesh that separates the carrion itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:7:8)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מְטַהֵר.** אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְאוֹקְמֵי מִלְּתֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אַהַךְ דְּסֵיפָא, דְּמִמָּה נַפְשָׁךְ, אִי מִיתָה עוֹשָׂה נִפּוּל לִטָּמֵא מִשּׁוּם אֵבָר מִן הַחַי, וְאִי אֵינָהּ עוֹשָׂה נִפּוּל לִטָּמֵא מִשּׁוּם אֵבָר מִן נְבֵלָה. הִלְכָּךְ עַל כָּרְחֲךָ רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אַרֵישָׁא קָאֵי, הָאֵבָר וְהַבָּשָׂר הַמְדֻלְדָּלִים בַּבְּהֵמָה מְטַמְּאִין טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִין בִּמְקוֹמָן, וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מְטַהֵר הָאֵבָר כָּל זְמַן שֶׁמְּעֻרֶה בְּבַעֲלֵי חַיִּים שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִין. וְטַעֲמָא דְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן, דְּאָמַר קְרָא (ויקרא יא) מִכָּל הָאֹכֶל אֲשֶׁר יֵאָכֵל, אֹכֶל שֶׁאַתָּה יָכוֹל לְהַאֲכִילוֹ לְנָכְרִי בְּהֶתֵּר קָרוּי אֹכֶל וּמְטַמֵּא טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים, לַאֲפוֹקֵי הָאֵבָר וְהַבָּשָׂר הַמְדֻלְדָּלִים שֶׁאֲסוּרִים לַנָּכְרִי מִשׁוּם אֵבָר מִן הַחַי וּמִשּׁוּם בָּשָׂר מִן הַחַי, שֶׁאֵינָן קְרוּיִין אֹכֶל וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִין טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר בִּשְׁתֵּיהֶן:


ורבי שמעון מטהר – for it is impossible to maintain the words of Rabbi Shimon with this concluding phrase, for whichever way you turn, if death makes the hanging limb to be considered as if detached to become defiled because of the limb from a living animal, but does not make a hanging limb to be considered as if detached to be defiled because of a limb from a carrion. Therefore, by force, Rabbi Shimon refers to the opening clause [of the Mishnah] - the limb and flesh that are dangling on the animal defile food defilement in their place, but Rabbi Shimon purifies the limb all the while that hat it is from its hide in living creatures that does not defile through food defilement. But the reason of Rabbi Shimon, that stated the Biblical verse (Leviticus 11:34): “As to any food that may be eaten, [it shall become impure if it came in contact with water],” food that you can feed to heathens with permission is called fod and it defiles the defilement of food, excluding the limb and the dangling flesh that are forbidden to a heathen because of the limb of a living animal and flesh from a living animal. They are not called food and they do not make the defilement of food. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Meir in both of these.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:8:1)


**הַמְדֻלְדָּלִים בָּאָדָם טְהוֹרִים.** דְּאָדָם כִּי יָמוּת כְּתִיב (במדבר יט):


המדולדלים באדם טהורים – for (Numbers 19:14): “when a person dies [in a tent]” it is written.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:8:2)


**מֵת הָאָדָם הַבָּשָׂר טָהוֹר.** דְּמִיתָה עוֹשָׂה נִפּוּל וּבָשָׂר שֶׁפֵּרֵשׁ מִן הָאָדָם הַחַי טָהוֹר:


מת האדם הבשר טהור – for death makes the dangling flesh and the the flesh that separated from the living human being pure–clean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:8:3)


**הָאֵבָר מְטַמֵּא מִשׁוּם אֵבָר מִן הַחַי.** וְדִין הָאֵבָר שֶׁפֵּרַשׁ מִן הָאָדָם הַחַי שֶׁהוּא מְטַמֵּא, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא אֵבָר שָׁלֵם מְטַמֵּא בְמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא וּבְאֹהֶל. אֲבָל כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר שֶׁפֵּרַשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ, אוֹ עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה שֶׁפֵּרַשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ, טָהוֹר. וְאֵבָר שֶׁפֵּרַשׁ מִן הַמֵּת וְחָזַר וּפֵרַשׁ מֵאוֹתוֹ אֵבָר כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר אוֹ עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה, טָמֵא:


האבר מטמא משום אבר מן החי – but the law of the limb that separates from the living human is what defiles, all the while that it is complete limb, it defiles through contact, and carrying and through tent-defilement. But an olive’s bulk of flesh that separated from him, or a bone the size of a barley-corn that separated from him, is pure–clean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 9:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/9:8:4)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מְטַהֵר.** בְּאֵבָר מִן הַמֵּת נַמִּי כְּמוֹ בְּאֵבָר מִן הַחַי. דְּסָבַר כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאֵבָר מִן הַחַי בָּשָׂר הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ אוֹ עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה הַפּוֹרֵשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ טָהוֹר, הָכִי נַמִּי בְּאֵבָר מִן הַמֵּת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:1:1)


**הַזְּרוֹעַ וְהַלְּחָיַיִם.** בָּאָרֶץ וּבְחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ. מִשּׁוּם דְּבָעֵי לְמִתְנֵי בְּחֻלִּין אֲבָל לֹא בְמֻקְדָּשִׁין, תָּנֵי לְכֻלְּהוּ:


הזרוע והלחיים. בארץ ובחוץ לארץ (the shoulder and the cheeks [and the stomach] – because it was needed to teach [in the Mishnah] with regard to unconsecrated products but not with that which is dedicated [to the Temple], he teaches all of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:1:2)


**בִּפְנֵי הַבַּיִת.** בִּזְמַן שֶׁבֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ קַיָּם:


בפני הבית – at the time when the Temple existed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:1:3)


**וָאֶתֵּן אֹתָם.** בְּחָזֶה וָשׁוֹק כְּתִיב. אוֹתָם מִעוּט הוּא, חָזֶה וָשׁוֹק אִין מִידִי אַחֲרִינָא לֹא:


ואתן אותם – (Leviticus 7:34: “and given them [to Aaron the priests and to his sons as their due from the Israelites for all time].”) – it is written regarding the shoulder and the cheeks. They are a a limitation: Dקואthe shoulder and the cheeks, yes; anything else, no.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:1)


**כָּל הַקֳּדָשִׁים שֶׁקָּדַם מוּם קָבוּעַ לְהֶקְדֵּשָׁן.** הֲרֵי הֵן כְּמַקְדִּישׁ עֵצִים וַאֲבָנִים לִדְמֵיהֶן, וְאֵין בָּהֶן קְדֻשַּׁת הַגּוּף:


כל הקדשים שקדם מדם קוע להקדישן – behold they are like one who dedicates–sanctifies wood and stones, and there isn’t in them an object which is consecrated as such.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:2)


**וְנִפְדּוּ חַיָּבִין בַּבְּכוֹרָה.** אִם יָלְדוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ. אֲבָל קֹדֶם פִּדְיוֹן לֹא, דִּקְדֻשַּׁת דָּמִים מַדְחָה מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה, שֶׁאֵין קְדֻשָּׁה חָלָה עַל קְדֻשָּׁה:


ונפדו חייבין בככורות – if they gave birth after hey were redeemed, but not prior to redemption, for that which is consecrated for its value is superseded from the first born [animal] for holiness does not takes effect on something already possessing holiness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:3)


**וּוְלָדָן וַחֲלָבָן מֻתָּר לְאַחַר פִּדְיוֹנָן.** אֲבָל מַקְדִּישׁ תְּמִימִים וְנַעֲשׂוּ בַעֲלֵי מוּמִין וְנִפְדּוּ, וְלָדָן אָסוּר כְּדִלְקַמָּן. וּבֵין רֵישָׁא דְקָתָנֵי מֻתָּר וּבֵין סֵיפָא דְקָתָנֵי אָסוּר מַיְרֵי כְּגוֹן דְּאִיעַבַּר לִפְנֵי פִדְיוֹנוֹ וְאִתְיַלִּיד לְאַחַר פִּדְיוֹנוֹ. וַחֲלָבָן נַמִּי דְּמַקְדִּישׁ תְּמִימִים וְנִפְדּוּ נַפְקָא לָן דְּאָסוּר דִּכְתִיב בִּפְסוּלֵי הַמֻּקְדָּשִׁין שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ תִּזְבַּח וְאָכַלְתָּ בָשָׂר, תִּזְבַּח וְלֹא גִיזָה, וְאָכַלְתָּ וְלֹא לִכְלָבֶיךָ, בָּשָׂר וְלֹא חָלָב:


וולדן וחלבן מותר לאחר פדיונן – but he dedicates–sanctifies [animals[ without physical blemish but if they became physically blemished and were redeemed, their offspring are forbidden as mentioned above. But whether regarding the first clause [of the Mishnah] where it is taught that is permitted or the latter clause [of the Mishnah] where it is taught that is forbidden, we are dealing with a case where the animal became pregnant prior to its redemption and that it gave birth after its redemption, and its milk also, when we sanctify [animals] without physical blemish and they were redeemed, he derives them that they are prohibited, as it is written regarding consecrated animals that are disqualified that were redeemed (Deuteronomy 12:15): “[But whatever you desire], you may slaughter and eat meat [in any of your settlements],’ you may slaughter, but not shear, you may eat, but not for your dogs, meat, but not milk–forbidden fat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:4)


**וְאֵין עוֹשִׂין תְּמוּרָה.** אֲפִלּוּ קֹדֶם פִּדְיוֹנָן. טוֹב מֵעִקָּרָא עוֹשֶׂה תְמוּרָה וַאֲפִלּוּ נַעֲשָׂה רַע אַחַר שֶׁהִקְדִּישׁוֹ, אֲבָל רַע מֵעִקָּרוֹ אֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה תְמוּרָה:


ואין עושין תמורה – [and you don’t make an exchange of one sacrificial animal for another] even prior to their redemption. [An animal] that is good initially one can make an exchange of one sacrificial animal for another, and even I it becomes bad after one has sanctified it, but an animal that is initially bad, one does not do an exchange of one sacrificial animal for another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:5)


**וְאִם מֵתוּ יִפָּדוּ.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָן רְאוּיִין אֶלָּא לַכְּלָבִים, וַאֲנַן קַיְמָא לָן דְּאֵין פּוֹדִין אֶת הַקֳּדָשִׁים לְהַאֲכִילָן לַכְּלָבִים, הָנֵי כְּעֵצִים בְּעַלְמָא נִינְהוּ וְלֹא נָחִית לְהוּ קְדֻשַּׁת הַגּוּף:


ואם מתו יפדו – and even though they ae not appropriate other than for dogs and we hold that we don’t redeem Holy Things to feed them to dogs. These are like mere wood and they do not remove the object which is consecrated which is irredeemable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:6)


**חוּץ מִן הַבְּכוֹר וְהַמַּעֲשֵׂר.** דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּקָדַם מוּמָן לְהֶקְדֵּשָׁן, חָלָה עֲלֵיהֶן קְדֻשָּׁה גְמוּרָה לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶם, אֶלָּא שֶׁאֵין כְּשֵׁרִים לִקָּרֵב, דִּבְכוֹר בָּרֶחֶם תָּלָה רַחֲמָנָא, לֹא שְׁנָא תָּם וְלֹא שְׁנָא בַּעַל מוּם קַדִּישׁ, תָּם קָרֵב, וּבַעַל מוּם נֶאֱכָל לַכֹּהֵן בְּמוּמוֹ. וּמַעְשַׂר בְּהֵמָה נַמִּי כְּתִיב בֵּיהּ (ויקרא כז) הָעֲשִׂירִי יִהְיֶה קֹדֶשׁ לַה'‏ לֹא יְבַקֵּר בֵּין טוֹב לָרַע. טוֹב תָּם, רַע בַּעַל מוּם:


חוץ מן הבכור והמעשר – and even though their physical blemishes preceded their dedication–sanctification, complete holiness takes effect for all their things, urt they are not fit to be sacrificed, for the All-Merciful assigned the firstborn in the womb, and it makes no difference whether it is pure or whether there is a physical defect, it is holy. He pure animal is offered up, but the one with a physical defect, the Kohen consumes with its defect, and the tithing of cattle is also written concerning it (Leviticus 27:32-33): “[All tithes of the herd or flock – of all that passes under the shepherd’s staff,] every thenty one -shalll be holy to the LOD. He must not look out for good as against bad, [or make substitution for it].” “Good” is an unblemished bull; bad is a bull with a physical defect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:7)


**אוֹ מוּם עוֹבֵר.** קוֹדֵם לְהֶקְדֵּשָׁן, דְּמוּם עוֹבֵר כְּמַאן דְּלֵיתֵיהּ דָּמֵי:


מום עובר קודם לשהקדישו – a passing physical defect is like he lacks it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:8)


**פְּטוּרִים מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה.** דִּכְתִיב בִּפְסוּלֵי הַמֻּקְדָּשִׁין שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ כַּצְּבִי וְכָאַיָּל, מַה צְּבִי וְאַיָּל פְּטוּרִים מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה, דְּהָא כָּל הַבְּכוֹר אֲשֶׁר יִוָּלֵד בִּבְקָרְךָ וּבְצֹאנֶךָ כְּתִיב (דברים טו), אַף פְּסוּלֵי הַמֻּקְדָּשִׁין שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ אֲפִלּוּ יָלְדוּ לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּפְדּוּ פְּטוּרִים מִן הַבְּכוֹרָה:


פטורים מן הבכורה – as it is written regarding consecrated animals that are disqualified that were redeemed (Deuteronomy 12:15): “ [The unclean and the clean alike may partake of it,] as of the gazelle and the deer.” Just as the gazelle and the deer are exempt from the law appertaining to first-born animals, because it is written (Deuteronomy 15:19): }You shall.consecrate to the LORD your God all male firstlings that are born in your herd and in your flock,” even consecrated animals that are disqualified that were redeemed even if they were born after they were redeemed, they are exemot from the law appertaining to the first-born.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:9)


**וּמִן הַמַּתָּנוֹת.** הַזְּרוֹעַ וְהַלְּחָיַיִם וְהַקֵּבָה:


המתנות – the shoulder, the cheeks and the stomach.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:10)


**וְלָדָן וַחֲלָבָן אָסוּר.** בִּדְאִיעַבַּר לִפְנֵי פִדְיוֹנוֹ וְאִתְיַלִּיד לְאַחַר פִּדְיוֹנוֹ מַיְרֵי, כְּדִפְרִישִׁית לְעֵיל. דְּאִי אִיעַבַּר וְאִתְיַלִּיד לְאַחַר פִּדְיוֹנוֹ, וְלַד צְבִי וְאַיָּל הוּא. וְאִי אִתְיַלִּיד לִפְנֵי פִדְיוֹנוֹ, אֲפִלּוּ קָדַם מוּמָן לְהֶקְדֵּשָׁן נַמִּי וְלָדָן אָסוּר:


וולדן וחלבן – we are speaking of when it became pregnant before its redemption and gave birth after its redemption, as was explained above. If it became pregnant and gave birth after its redemption, its offspring is a deer and a ram. But if it did not give birth prior to its redemption, even if its physical defect preceded its dedication–sanctification, also, its offspring are forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:11)


**וְהַשּׁוֹחֲטָן בַּחוּץ חַיָּב.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵינָן רְאוּיִן לְפֶתַח אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד דְּהָא בַּעֲלֵי מוּמִין נִינְהוּ, וּתְנַן הָרָאוּי לְפֶתַח אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד חַיָּבִין עָלָיו בַּחוּץ וְשֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לִפְנִים אֵין חַיָּבִים עָלָיו בַּחוּץ, מוֹקְמִינַן לָהּ לְמַתְנִיתִין בְּדֻקִּין שֶׁבָּעַיִן וְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא הִיא דְּאָמַר אִם עָלוּ לֹא יֵרְדּוּ, הִלְכָּךְ הוֹאִיל וּבִפְנִים לֹא יֵרְדּוּ, חַיָּבִין עֲלֵיהֶם בַּחוּץ אִם שְׁחָטָן קֹדֶם פִּדְיוֹנָן:


והשוחטן בחוץ חייב – and even though they are not worthy for the entrance to the Tent of Meeting because they have physical defects, and it is taught in the Mishnah (actually a Baraita, Tractate Yoma 63b and Tractate Temurah 6b): That which is worthy for the entrance to the Tent of Meeting, we are liable for [if slaughtered] outside [the Temple courtyard], and that which is not worthy inside, we are not liable for [if slaughtered] outside, Our Mishnah establishes it as a veiled or withered spot in the eye–cataract and that it is [the opinion of] Rabbi Akiva who stated (see Tractate Zevakhim, Chapter 9, Mishnah 3) that [regarding animals that are blemished], if they have gone up, they should not go down; therefore, for since they are inside, they do not go down, and we are liable for them outside if he slaughtered them prior to their redemption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:12)


**וְעוֹשִׂין תְּמוּרָה.** קֹדֶם פִּדְיוֹנָן. דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) טוֹב בְּרַע אוֹ רַע בְּטוֹב:


ועושין תמורה – prior to their redemption, as it is written (Leviticus 27:10): “[One may not exchange or substitute another for it,] either good for bad, or bad for good; [if one does substitute one animal for another, the thing vowed and its substitute shall both be holy].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:13)


**וְאִם מֵתוּ.** מֵעַצְמָן:


ואם מתו – from their own accord.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:2:14)


**יִקָּבֵרוּ.** דְּאֵין פּוֹדִין אֶת הַקֳּדָשִׁים לְהַאֲכִילָן לַכְּלָבִים:


יקברו – and we don’t redeem the Holy Things to feed them to the dogs.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:1)


**בְּכוֹר שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בְּמֵאָה.** מַתְנִיתִין אַיְרֵי בִּבְכוֹר שֶׁבָּא לְיַד כֹּהֵן וְנָפַל בּוֹ מוּם בְּיַד כֹּהֵן וּמְכָרוֹ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמוּמוֹ. דְּאִי בִּבְכוֹר בְּיַד יִשְׂרָאֵל קֹדֶם שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ לַכֹּהֵן, אַמַּאי פּוֹטְרִים אֶת כֻּלָּן מִמַּתְּנוֹת כְּהֻנָּה, לֵימָא לֵיהּ כֹּהֵן, אִי בְּכוֹר הוּא כֻּלַּהּ דִּידִי הוּא, אִי לֹא בְכוֹר הוּא הַב לִי מַתְּנְתָאֵי. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁכְּבָר בָּא לְיַד כֹּהֵן וּמְכָרוֹ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמוּמוֹ וְנִתְעָרֵב בְּמֵאָה, פּוֹטְרִים אֶת כֻּלָּן מִמַּתְּנוֹת כְּהֻנָּה, דְּכָל חַד וְחַד יָכוֹל לִדְחוֹת אֶת עַצְמוֹ וְלוֹמַר לַכֹּהֵן, שֶׁלִּי בְּכוֹר הוּא, שֶׁמְּכָרוֹ הַכֹּהֵן וּפָטוּר מִן הַמַּתָּנוֹת, דְּמִן הַבְּכוֹר אֵין נוֹתְנִים מַתָּנוֹת, שֶׁאֵין קְדֻשָּׁה חָלָה עַל קְדֻשָּׁה:


בכור שנתערב במאה – Our Mishnah is dealing with a firstling that came into the hand of a Kohen and a physical defect befell it while in the Kohen’s hand and he sold it to an Israelite with its physical defect. For if it had been a firstling in the hand of an Israelite prior to his giving it to the Kohen, why would we exempt all of them from priestly gifts? The Kohen should say to him: “If it is a firstling, all of it is mine, and if it is not a firstling, give me my gifts.” But, if had already come into the hand of the Kohen and he sold it with its physical defect to an Israelite, and it became mixed up with one hundred [unconsecrated animals], we exempt all of them from [being given] as priestly gifts, for each and every [person] can set himself aside and say to the Kohen: “Mine is the firstling, that he Kohen sold” and he is exempt from the gifts, and from the firstling, we don’t give gits for there can be no sanctity that occur to something [already] holy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:2)


**אֶחָד שׁוֹחֵט אֶת כֻּלָּן פּוֹטְרִין לוֹ אֶחָד.** שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא הַבְּכוֹר אֶחָד מֵהֶן, וְיָכוֹל לוֹמַר זֶה הוּא:


אחד שוחט את כולן פוטרים לו אחד – that it is impossible that the firstiling would not be one of them, and he is able to say, “this is it.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:3)


**צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּרְשֹׁם.** שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה בוֹ סִימָן. שֶׁיָּבִינוּ הַכֹּל שֶׁיֵּשׁ לְכֹהֵן אוֹ לְנָכְרִי שֻׁתָּפוּת בָּהּ:


צריך שירשום – that he should make on it a sign, that everyone will understand that the Kohen or the heathen have a joint-ownership in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:4)


**וְאִם אָמַר לוֹ.** אִם אָמַר כֹּהֵן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל פָּרָה זוֹ אֲנִי מוֹכֵר לְךָ חוּץ מִן הַמַּתָּנוֹת שֶׁבָּהּ:


ואם אמר לו – if the Kohen told the Israelite: “I am selling you this cow, except for the [priestly] gifts that are within it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:5)


**פָּטוּר.** יִשְׂרָאֵל מִן הַמַּתָּנוֹת:


פטור – the Israelite [is exempt] from giving the [priestly] gifts.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:6)


**אָמַר לוֹ.** יִשְׂרָאֵל לַחֲבֵרוֹ טַבָּח, מְכֹר לִי בְּנֵי מֵעֶיהָ שֶׁל פָּרָה זוֹ:


אמר לו – An Israelite [said] to his fellow, a slaughterer: “See me the intestines of this cow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:7)


**וְהָיוּ בָהֶן מַתָּנוֹת.** הַקֵּבָה, שֶׁהִיא אַחַת מִן הַמַּתָּנוֹת:


והיו בהן מתנות -, the stomach, which is one of the [priestly] gifts.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:8)


**נוֹתְנָן.** לוֹקֵחַ זֶה לַכֹּהֵן:


נותנו – takes this to the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:9)


**וְאֵין הַמּוֹכֵר מְנַכֶּה לוֹ מִן הַדָּמִים.** שֶׁהֲרֵי הָיָה יוֹדֵעַ הַלּוֹקֵחַ שֶׁמַּתָּנוֹת שָׁם, וְזֶה לֹא מָכַר לוֹ אֶת הַקֵּבָה:


ואין המוכר מנכה לו מן הדמים – for the purchaser knows that the [priestly] gifts are there, and this one did not sell him the stomach.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:3:10)


**לָקַח הֵימֶנּוּ בְמִשְׁקָל.** כָּךְ וְכָךְ הַלִּטְרָא, וְשָׁקַל לוֹ הַקֵּבָה. נוֹתְנָהּ לוֹ לוֹקֵחַ לַכֹּהֵן, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַגְּזֵלָה אֶצְלוֹ וְצָרִיךְ לְהָשִׁיב, וְהַטַּבָּח יְנַכֶּה לוֹ מִן הַדָּמִים, שֶׁהֲרֵי מָכַר דָּבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ שֶׁלּוֹ. וְאִם עָבַר הַשּׁוֹחֵט וְלֹא נָתַן מַתָּנוֹת מִן הַבְּהֵמָה, לֹא נֶאֱסַר הַבָּשָׂר בַּאֲכִילָה, אֲבָל מְנַדִּין אוֹתוֹ. וַאֲפִלּוּ כֹּהֵן שֶׁפָּטוּר מִן הַמַּתָּנוֹת, אִם הוּא טַבָּח שֶׁשּׁוֹחֵט וּמוֹכֵר בַּשּׁוּק, מַמְתִּינִין לוֹ שְׁתַּיִם וְשָׁלֹשׁ שַׁבָּתוֹת, מִכָּאן וָאֵילָךְ מוֹצִיאִין מִמֶּנּוּ מַתָּנוֹת וְנוֹתְנִין אוֹתָן לְכֹהֲנִים אֲחֵרִים. וְאִם קָבַע בֵּית מִטְבָּחַיִם, אֵין מַמְתִּינִים לוֹ אֶלָּא מוֹצִיאִין מִמֶּנּוּ מִיָּד, וְאִם לֹא רָצָה לִתֵּן מְנַדִּין אוֹתוֹ:


לקח הימנו במשקל – So many Litra (pounds)., and he weighed for him the stomach and the purchaser gave it to the Kohen, for it was something stolen with him and he needed to return it, and the butcher would deduct for him from the monies, for he sold a thing that is not his. But if the ritual slaughterer sinned and did not give the [priestly] gifts from the animal,the eat is not forbidden for eating. But we excommunicate him. For even a Kohen who is exempt from the [priestly] gifts, if he is a butcher, that slaughters and sells it in the marketplace, we wait for him two or three weeks, and from then onwards, we remove from it the [priestly] gifts and give them to other Kohanim. But if he established a butcher house, we don’t wait for him, but rather remove it from him immediately bbut if he did not want to give them, we excommunicate him.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:1)


**מִן הַפֶּרֶק שֶׁל אַרְכֻּבָּה.** הַנִּמְכֶּרֶת עִם הָרֹאשׁ:


מן הפרק של ארכובה (from the joint of the knee to the shoulder-blade of the forefoot) – which is sold with the head.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:2)


**עַד כַּף שֶׁל יָד.** עֶצֶם רָחָב שֶׁל כָּתֵף שֶׁקּוֹרִין אספלדו"ן. וְהֵן שְׁנֵי עֲצָמוֹת, הָעֶצֶם הָאֶמְצָעִי דְּהַיְנוּ מִן הַפֶּרֶק שֶׁל אַרְכֻּבָּה הַנִּמְכֶּרֶת עִם הָרֹאשׁ עַד מְקוֹם שֶׁכְּנֶגְדּוֹ בְּגָמָל נִכָּר, וְהָעֶצֶם הָעֶלְיוֹן הַמְחֻבָּר לַגּוּף. וְהַזְּרוֹעַ הַיְמִינִי בִּלְבָד הוּא שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים לַכֹּהֵן, דִּכְתִיב (דברים יח) וְנָתַן לַכֹּהֵן הַזְּרוֹעַ, הַמְיֻמָּן שֶׁבַּזְּרוֹעַ:


עד כף של יד (until the palm of the hand) – a wide bone of the shoulder that we call ASPALDONE. And they are two bones. The middle bone which is from the joint of the knee to the shoulder blade of the forefoot which is sold with the head until the place that is opposite in the known camel. And the upper bone that is attached to the body. And the right foreleg alone is what they give to the Kohen, as it is written (Deuteronomy 18:3): “[he] must give the shoulder, [the cheeks and the stomach] to the priest,”ץ the dexterous part of the forearm.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:3)


**וְהוּא שֶׁל נָזִיר.** כְּלוֹמַר, וְכֵן זְרוֹעַ בְּשֵׁלָה הָאָמוּר בַּנָּזִיר, כָּךְ נִטֶּלֶת:


והוא של נזיר – meaning to say, and similarly (Numbers 6:19): “[The priest shall take] the shoulder [of the ram] when it has been boiled,” that is mentioned regarding the Nazir, such is taken


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:4)


**וּכְנֶגְדּוֹ בָרֶגֶל שׁוֹק.** הָאָמוּר בִּשְׁלָמִים נַמִּי הֲוֵי שְׁנֵי עֲצָמוֹת מִן הַפֶּרֶק שֶׁל אַרְכֻּבָּה עַד בּוּקָא דְאַטְמָא, דְּהַיְנוּ כָּל הַיָּרֵךְ:


וכנגדו ברגל שוק – that is stated regarding the offering of well-being–peace offering – these are two bones from the joint of the knee to the shoulder blade of the forefoot until the thickest part of the flank (i.e., the thigh, hip) which is all of the thigh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:5)


**סֹבֶךְ שֶׁל רָגֶל.** הַיְנוּ פֶּרֶק שֶׁבֵּין הַקּוּלִית וְעֶצֶם הָאֶמְצָעִי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


סובך של רגל (the fleshy part of the leg–calf) – that is the joint that is between the thigh-bone and the middle bone. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:6)


**הַפֶּרֶק שֶׁל לְחִי.** אֵצֶל הַצְּדָעִים. וְחוֹתֵךְ כְּלַפֵּי מַטָּה:


הפרק של לחי – near the temples and cuts it towards downward.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 10:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/10:4:7)


**עַד פִּקָּה שֶׁל גַּרְגָּרֶת.** עַד שִׁיפּוּי כוֹבַע שֶׁהִיא פַּקְעִיתָהּ וּפִתְחָהּ שֶׁל קָנֶה, דְּהַיְנוּ לְחָיַיִם הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת עִם הַלָּשׁוֹן. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, פִּקָּה שֶׁל גַּרְגֶּרֶת טַבַּעַת הַגְּדוֹלָה שֶׁל קָנֶה שֶׁעֲשׂוּיָה עֲגֻלָּה כְּפִקָּה. וּפִקָּה הִיא עֲגֻלָּה כְּכַדּוּר וּבְתוֹכָהּ נֶקֶב שֶׁתּוֹחֲבוֹת הַנָּשִׁים בַּפֶּלֶךְ לְהַכְבִּידוֹ כְּשֶׁטֹּווֹת בּוֹ:


עד פיקח של גרגרת (protruding thyroid cartilage) – until the thyroid cartilage–Adam’s Apple which is the upper ring and the opening of the windpipe which is the lower jaw with the tongue. Another explanation: thyroid cartrilage is a large ring of the windpipe that is made round like a protruding cartilage, and the protruding cartilage is round like a ball and within it is a hole hat the women insert in the spindle to make it heavy when they spin with it.



## Chapter 11


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:1)


**רֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז.** כָּל הַגּוֹזֵז צֹאנוֹ וַאֲפִלּוּ גּוֹזְזָן מֵאָה פְעָמִים נוֹתֵן מִן הַגִּיזָה מַתָּנָה לַכֹּהֵן:


ראשית הגז – Whomever shears his sheep and even if he shears them one-hundred times, he gives from the shearing a gift to the Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:2)


**נוֹהֵג בָּאָרֶץ וּבְחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ.** וְהָאִידְנָא נָהוּג עַלְמָא כְּרַבִּי אִלָּעִאי, דְּאָמַר רֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז אֵינוֹ נוֹהֵג אֶלָּא בָאָרֶץ. דְּיָלֵיף נְתִינָה נְתִינָה מִתְּרוּמָה, מַה תְּרוּמָה אֵינָהּ נוֹהֶגֶת אֶלָּא בָאָרֶץ אַף רֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז אֵינוֹ נוֹהֵג אֶלָּא בָאָרֶץ. וְרַבּוֹתַי הוֹרוּ בַּמַּתָּנוֹת נַמִּי דְּאֵין נוֹהֲגוֹת אֶלָּא בָאָרֶץ, דְּרֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז וְהַמַּתָּנוֹת שָׁוִין הֵן. וְלֹא הוֹדוּ לָהֶן שְׁאָר חַכְמֵי הַדּוֹר:


נוהג בארץ ובחוצה לארץ – but today, the practice, of the world according to Rabbi Elai, who said, that the first of the fleece is not practiced other than in the Land [of Israel]. For it derives [through an analogy], from the laws of Terumah–heave offering (see Talmud Hullin 136a: See Deuteronomy 18:4: “You shall also give him….the first shearing of your sheep–וראשית גז צאנך תתן-לו and the fact that Terumah must take place in the land of Israel – Deuteronomy 26:2: “which you harvest from the land that the LORD your God is giving you”–אשר תביא מארצך אשר ה' אלהיך נתן לך ). Just as Terumah–heave-offering is not practiced other than in the Land [of Israel], so the first of the fleece is not practiced other than in the Land [of Israel]. But my teachers–masters taught that the gifts also are not practiced other than in the Land [of Israel], for the first of the fleece and the gifts are equivalent. But the rest of the Sages of that generation did not agree with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:3)


**בְּמֻקְדָּשִׁים.** אֲפִלּוּ קָדְשֵׁי בֶדֶק הַבַּיִת. מִשּׁוּם דִּכְתִיב גֵּז צֹאנְךָ, וְלֹא שֶׁל הֶקְדֵּשׁ:


במקודשים – even the offerings for Temple repair, because it is written (Deuteronomy 18:4): “the shearing of your sheep,” but not that of which is dedicated to the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:4)


**נוֹהֲגִים בְּבָקָר וּבְצֹאן.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים יח) אִם שׁוֹר אִם שֶׂה:


נוהגים בבקר ובצאן – as it is written (Deuteronomy 18:3): “[Everyone who offers a sacrifice,] whether an ox or a sheep, [must give he shoulder, the cheeks, and the stomach to the priest].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:5)


**וּבְמֻעָט.** אֲפִלּוּ לֹא שָׁחַט אֶלָּא אַחַת:


ובמועט – even if he only slaughtered one [animal].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:1:6)


**אֶלָּא בִרְחֵלוֹת.** כְּתִיב הָכָא (שם) גֵּז צֹאנְךָ, וּכְתִיב הָתָם (איוב לא) וּמִגֵּז כְּבָשַׂי יִתְחַמֵּם, מַה לְּהַלָּן כְּבָשִׂים אַף כָּאן כְּבָשִׂים:


אלא ברחלות – It is written here (Deuteronomy 18:4): “the shearing of your sheep” and it is written there (Job 31:20): “As he warmed himself with the shearing of my sheep.” Just as there it is sheep, even here it is sheep.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:1)


**וּשְׁתֵּי צֹאן.** אַלְמָא תַּרְתֵּי מִקְרֵי צֹאן:


ושתי צאן – so we see that two are called sheep.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:2)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר חָמֵשׁ צֹאן עֲשׂוּיוֹת.** שֶׁמְּעַשּׂוֹת אֶת בַּעֲלֵיהֶן וְאוֹמְרוֹת לוֹ קוּם עֲשֵׂה מִצְוָה חֲדָשָׁה, שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה מִצְוָה עָלָיו בְּפָחוֹת מִכֵּן. וְהַיְנוּ עַל כָּרְחֲךָ רֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז, דִּכְתִיב בֵּיהּ צֹאן דְּמַשְׁמַע מְרֻבֶּה. דְּאִלּוּ בְּכוֹר כְּתִיב בְּכוֹר שׁוֹר, דְּמַשְׁמַע אֲפִלּוּ חַד. וְכֵן מַתָּנוֹת אִם שׁוֹר אִם שֶׂה:


שנאמר חמש צאן עשויות – that they order their husbands say to him: “Arise and perform a new Commandment that was not commanded upon him that is less than this. And this corresponding per force, the first shearing, as it is written [in the Torah] regarding (Deuteronomy 18:4): “[the first shearing of] your sheep,” which implies many, whereas regarding the ox it is written (Numbers 18:17): “But the firstlings of cattle–בכור שור, [sheep or goats ]” (singular construction in the Hebrew) which implies even one. And similar gifts (Deuteronomy 18:3): “whether an ox or a sheep–אם שור אם שה .”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:3)


**מָנֶה מָנֶה וּפְרָס.** כָּל אַחַת מָנֶה וָחֵצִי. וּבְצִיר מֵהָכִי לָא חֲשִׁיב גֵּז, שֶׁזּוֹ פָּחוּת שֶׁבַּגִּיזוֹת:


מנה מנה ופרס – each one is a Maneh and one-half. But less than this is not considered shearing, for this is the least of shearing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:4)


**בְּכָל שֶׁהֵן.** לָאו דַּוְקָא, דִּבְפָחוֹת מִשִּׁשִּׁים סְלָעִים לֹא מִחַיַּב בְּרֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז. אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דְּרַבִּי דּוֹסָא יְהִיב שִׁעוּרָא רַבָּה, קָרֵי תַּנָּא לְשִׁעוּרָא זוּטָא דְרַבָּנָן כָּל שֶׁהֵן. וְהַסֶּלַע מִשְׁקָלוֹ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה מָעִים, וּמִשְׁקַל כָּל מָעָה שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר גַּרְעִינֵי שְׂעוֹרָה:


בכל שהוא – not exactly, for since with less than sixty Selaim, he is not liable for the first of the fleece, but rather, since Rabbi Dosa [ben Harkinas] gives a large measure, the Tanna–teacher [of the Mishnah] calls for a small measurement according to the Rabbis of “however much they produce.” But the Selah – its weight is twenty-four Ma’ah. And the weight of each Ma’ah is sixteen stones–kernels of barley.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:5)


**כַּמָּה הוּא נוֹתֵן לוֹ.** הַבָּא לְחַלֵּק רֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ, לֹא יִפְחוֹת לְכָל כֹּהֵן מִמִּשְׁקַל חָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים צֶמֶר:


כמה הוא נותן לו – he comes to divide the first of the fleece that is in his hand, he should not give less than the weight of five Selaim of wool to each Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:6)


**מְלֻבָּן.** וְלֹא שֶׁיִּהְיֶה חַיָּב הַיִּשְׂרָאֵל לְלַבְּנוֹ, אֶלָּא שֶׁיִּתֵּן לוֹ שִׁעוּר מִן הַצֶּמֶר הַצּוֹאִי, דְּהַיְנוּ שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְלֻבָּן, עַד שֶׁכְּשֶׁיְּלַבְּנֶנּוּ הַכֹּהֵן יַעֲמֹד עַל מִשְׁקַל חָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים צֶמֶר מְלֻבָּן:


מלובן – but the Israelite will not be liable to bleach it, but rather, he will give him a measure from the dirty wool, which is that it is not bleached until the Kohen will, that is, which is not bleached until that the Kohen will bleach it, he will estimate the weight of five Selaim of bleached wool.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:7)


**כְּדֵי.** שֶׁיּוּכַל: **לַעֲשׂוֹת מִמֶּנּוּ בֶגֶד קָטָן.** הָרָאוּי לְשֵׁרוּת. וּמַאי נִיהוּ, אַבְנֵט. מִדְּסָמַךְ קְרָא אַחַר רֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז מִיָּד, כִּי בוֹ בָּחַר ה'‏ אֱלֹהֶיךָ מִכָּל שְׁבָטֶיךָ לַעֲמֹד לְשָׁרֵת, שָׁמְעִינַן מִנַּהּ דְּהָכִי קָאָמַר, תֵּן לוֹ מֵרֵאשִׁית גֵּז צֹאנְךָ כְּדֵי שֶׁיּוּכַל לַעֲשׂוֹת בֶּגֶד לַעֲמֹד לְשָׁרֵת. וְהַקָּטָן שֶׁבַּבְּגָדִים הָרָאוּי לְשֵׁרוּת הוּא אַבְנֵט, וְהוּא נַעֲשֶׂה מֵחָמֵשׁ סְלָעִים צֶמֶר מְלֻבָּן. וְרֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז אֵין לוֹ שִׁעוּר מִן הַתּוֹרָה, וּמִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים שֶׁלֹּא יִפְחוֹת מֵאֶחָד מִשִּׁשִּׁים. וְאֵין חַיָּב בְּרֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז עַד שֶׁיִּגְזֹז חָמֵשׁ צֹאן, וְתִהְיֶה גִזַּת כָּל אַחַת מֵחֲמִשְׁתָּן אֵין פָּחוֹת מִי"ב סֶלַע, שֶׁאִם הָיְתָה גִיזַת אַחַת מֵהֶן פָּחוֹת מִי"ב סֶלַע אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחֲמִשְׁתָּן גּוֹזְזוֹת יוֹתֵר מִשִּׁשִּׁים סְלָעִים הֲרֵי זֶה פָּטוּר:


כדי שיוכל לעשות ממנו בגד קטן – that is worthy for service [in the Temple]. And what is it? A belt–אבנט . Because the Biblical verse supported [by being adjacent to] after [the law of] the first shearing (Deuteronomy 18:5): “For the LORD your God has chosen him and his descendants, out of all your tribes, to be in attendance for service [in the name of the LORD for all time[.” We learn from it, that this is what it says: Give him from the first shearing of your sheep in order that he can make clothing to be in attendance for service. And the smallest of the clothing that is appropriate for [Divine] service is the belt, and it iis made from five Selaim of bleached wool. But the first shearing has no fixed measure from the Torah. But from the words of the Scribes, it should not be less than one sixtieth. But there is no obligation for the first shearing until he shears five sheep, and that the shearing of each one of these five, there is not less than twelve Selah, for if there was one of them less than twelve Selah, even though the five of them (i.e., sheep), shears more than sixty Selaim, this would be execmpt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:8)


**עַד שֶׁצְּבָעוֹ.** בְּעָלָיו קֹדֶם שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ:


עד שצבאו – its owners [would dye it] prior to giving it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:9)


**פָּטוּר.** מִלִּתְּנוֹ עוֹד. דְּקַנְיֵהּ בְּשִׁנּוּי, וַהֲוָה לֵיהּ כְּמַזִּיק מַתְּנוֹת כְּהֻנָּה אוֹ שֶׁאֲכָלָן, דְּפָטוּר:


פטור – from giving him more. For it was acquired through change, and it is like he damages the gifts of priesthood or that he consumes them, that he is exempt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:10)


**וְלֹא צְבָעוֹ.** אֵין זֶה שִׁנּוּי וְאַכַּתִּי בְּעֵינֵיהּ הוּא:


ולא צבאו – this is not a change, and yet, in its natural form.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:11)


**הַלּוֹקֵחַ גֵּז צֹאנוֹ שֶׁל נָכְרִי.** כְּשֶׁהוּא מְחֻבָּר לַצֹּאן, פָּטוּר. דְּגֵז צֹאנְךָ כְּתִיב, וְהַאי צֹאן לָאו דִּידֵיהּ הוּא, וְאַצֹּאן קָפֵיד רַחֲמָנָא וְלָאו אַגִּיזִין דִּידֵיהּ:


הלוקח גז צאנו של נכרי – when it is attached to the sheep, he is exempt, for it is written (Deuteronomy 18:4): “the first shearing of your sheep.” But this sheep is not his, and the All-Merciful was stringent with sheep, and not on its shearing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:12)


**אִם שִׁיֵּר הַמּוֹכֵר הַמּוֹכֵר חַיָּב.** דְּאָמַר לֵיהּ לוֹקֵחַ מַתָּנָה דְכֹהֵן גַּבָּךְ הִיא:


אם שייר המוכרת המוכר חייב – for the purchaser said to him, the gift of the Kohanim is with you.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:13)


**וְאִם לָאו הַלּוֹקֵחַ חַיָּב.** דְּאָמַר לֵיהּ מוֹכֵר מַתָּנָה דְכֹהֵן לֹא זַבַּנִי לָךְ:


ואם לאו הלוקח חייב – for the seller said to him, “I have not sold you the gift for the Priest.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:14)


**שְׁחוּפוֹת.** לֹא שְׁחוֹרוֹת וְלֹא לְבָנוֹת:


שחופות – neither black nor white.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 11:2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/11:2:15)


**זֶה נוֹתֵן לְעַצְמוֹ.** הַלּוֹקֵחַ נוֹתֵן לְעַצְמוֹ עַל מַה שֶּׁלָּקַח, וְהַמּוֹכֵר נוֹתֵן לְעַצְמוֹ עַל מַה שֶּׁשִּׁיֵּר אֶצְלוֹ. דְּגִיזַת הַלְּבָנוֹת טוֹבָה מִשֶּׁל שְׁחוּפוֹת. וְכֵן זְכָרִים וּנְקֵבוֹת גִּיזַת הַזְּכָרִים קָשָׁה וְהַאי דִנְקֵבוֹת רַכִּיךְ:


זה נותן לעצמו – the purchaser gives to himself on what he purchased, and the seller gives to him on what remains with him. For the shearing of the white–bleached ones is better than that of indistinct color–grey. And similarly, regarding males and females, the shearing of the males is hard and that of the females is soft.



## Chapter 12


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:1:1)


**שִׁלּוּחַ הַקֵּן.** אֲבָל לֹא בְמֻקְדָּשִׁין. מַשְׁכַּחַת מֻקְדָּשִׁין דְּאִלּוּ הָיוּ חֻלִּין הָיוּ חַיָּבִים בְּשִׁלּוּחַ הַקֵּן, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ עוֹף וְהִקְדִּישׁוֹ כְּשֶׁהוּא בִרְשׁוּתוֹ לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת, וּבָרַח, וּמְצָאוֹ אַחַר כָּךְ רוֹבֵץ עַל הַקֵּן וְהִכִּירוֹ. אִי נַמִּי, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהִקְדִּישׁ גּוֹזָלוֹת שֶׁל שׁוֹבָכוֹ לַמִּזְבֵּחַ לְעוֹלַת נְדָבָה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ כְּשֶׁגָּדְלוּ אוֹתָן גּוֹזָלוֹת בָּרְחוּ וְיָצְאוּ וְקִנְּנוּ בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר, דְּמֵעִקָּרָא כִּי אַקְדְּשִׁינְהוּ דִּידֵיהּ הֲווּ וְחָל הֶקְדֵּשׁ עֲלַיְהוּ, וְהַשְׁתָּא דִּמְצָאָן לָאו מְזֻמָּן הוּא, וְאִי הֲווּ חֻלִּין הֲווּ מִחַיְּבֵי:


שלוח הקן. אבל לא במקודשין –consecrated [birds] are found, for if they were non-holy, they would be liable for letting [the dam] go forth from the nest, such as the example where he had a bird and he consecrated it while it was in his domain for keeping the Temple in repair, but it escaped and he found it afterwards lying on the nest and he recognized it. Alternatively, such as the example when he consecrated pigeons of [his dove-cote] to the Altar for a free-will burnt offering, and afterwards when these pigeons grew up, they escaped and built a nest in another place, for at the outset, when he sanctified them, they were his, and sacred Temple property takes effect upon them, and now that he found them, they are not designated–at his disposal–captive but if they were profane, they would be נו


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:1:2)


**וְאֵינוֹ נוֹהֵג אֶלָּא בְשֶׁאֵינוֹ מְזֻמָּן.** דִּכְתִיב כִּי יִקָּרֵא, פְּרָט לִמְזֻמָּן:


ואינו נוהג אלא בשאינו מזומן – as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:5): “[If, along the road,] you chance upon [a bird’s nest], excluding one that is at your disposal [in your courtyard](see Sifre Deuteronomy 227).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:1:3)


**שֶׁקִּנְּנוּ בְפַרְדֵּס.** שֶׁמָּרְדוּ וְיָצְאוּ מִן הַבַּיִת, וְאֵינָן חוֹזְרוֹת לַבַּיִת, וְנַעֲשׂוּ מִדְבָּרִיּוֹת. וּפַרְדֵּס לָאו מְזֻמָּן הוּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁיְּכוֹלוֹת לִבְרֹחַ:


שקננו בפרדס – that they rebelled and went out from the house, and they don’t return to the house and they become belonging to the desert. And an orchard is not designated because they can flee–escape.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:1:4)


**הַרְדְּסִיאוֹת.** שֶׁדַּרְכָּן לִגָּדֵל עִם בְּנֵי אָדָם. וְעַל שֵׁם הוֹרְדוֹס הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁהָיָה מִתְעַסֵּק בְּגִדּוּלָן נִקְרְאוּ הַרְדְּסִיאוֹת עַל שְׁמוֹ:


הרדסיות – for it is their manner to be raised with people and in the name of King Herod who was engaged in raising them, they are called, Herodian doves, by his name.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:2:1)


**עוֹף טָמֵא פָּטוּר מִלְּשַׁלֵּחַ.** דִּכְתִיב קַן צִפּוֹר, עוֹף מַשְׁמַע בֵּין טָהוֹר בֵּין טָמֵא, צִפּוֹר טָהוֹר וְלֹא טָמֵא:


עוף טמא פטור מלשלח – as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:6): “a bird’s nest.” A “bird” implies whether pure or impure, a pure bird, not an impure one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:2:2)


**עוֹף טָמֵא רוֹבֵץ עַל בֵּיצֵי עוֹף טָהוֹר.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּמִינָא דְאֶפְרוֹחִים בַּר שִׁלּוּחַ הוּא, פָּטוּר. דְּקַן צִפּוֹר בָּעִינַן, שֶׁתְּהֵא הָאֵם הַמְקַנֶּנֶת טְהוֹרָה:


עוף טמא רובץ על ביצי עוף טהור – even though that this species of pigeons are ones that are sent out from the nest], he is exempt, for we require, “a bird’s nest” (Deuteronomy 22:5) that the mother who makes a nest is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:2:3)


**וְעוֹף טָהוֹר הָרוֹבֵץ עַל בֵּיצֵי עוֹף טָמֵא פָּטוּר.** דְּאָמַר קְרָא וְאֶת הַבָּנִים תִּקַּח לָךְ, לְךָ וְלֹא לִכְלָבֶיךָ:


ועוף טהור הרבץ על ביצי עוף טמא – for Scripture states (Deuteronomy 22:7): “and take only the young,” for you, but not for your dogs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:2:4)


**קוֹרֵא.** בַּעֲרָבִי קוֹרִין לוֹ שונא"ר, וּבְלַעַ"ז פרדי"ז. וְדַרְכּוֹ שֶׁהַזָּכָר רוֹבֵץ עַל הַבֵּיצִים כְּמוֹ הַנְּקֵבָה, הִלְכָּךְ רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְחַיֵּב לְשַׁלֵּחַ הַזָּכָר. אֲבָל בִּשְׁאָר עוֹפוֹת מוֹדֶה רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר שֶׁזָּכָר פָּטוּר, דְּאֵם אָמַר רַחֲמָנָא, וְלֹא אָב. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


קורא – In Arabic, we call him “Sonar,” and in the foreign language, “Pardiz,” for it is the manner of the male [bird] to lie down on the eggs like the female [bird], therefore, Rabbi Eliezer obligates to send away the male [bird], but with other birds, Rabbi Eliezer admits that the male is exempt, for the All Merciful stated, “mother” (Deuteronomy 22:7), and not the father, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:3:1)


**בִּזְמַן שֶׁכְּנָפֶיהָ נוֹגְעוֹת בַּקֵּן חַיָּב לְשַׁלֵּחַ.** דְּאָמַר קְרָא רוֹבֶצֶת, וְלֹא מְעוֹפֶפֶת. וּמִדְּכָתַב רוֹבֶצֶת וְלֹא כָתַב יוֹשֶׁבֶת, שְׁמַע מִנַּהּ דְּאִם כְּנָפֶיהָ נוֹגְעוֹת בַּקֵּן חַיֶּבֶת:


בזמן שכנפיה נוגעות בקן חייב לשלח – as Scripture states (Deuteronomy 22:6), “sitting over [the fledglings or the eggs]” but not flying. But since it is written, “sitting over,” and it didn’t write, “sitting,” we learn from it that if its wings touch the nest, she is liable to be sent away.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:3:2)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר שַׁלֵּחַ.** לְעוֹלָם מַשְׁמַע:


שנאמר שלח – and it implies, forever.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:3:3)


**נָטַל אֶת הַבָּנִים וְכוּ'.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּנָטַל אֶת הַבָּנִים הֲוֵי לֵיהּ קֵן מְזֻמָּן:


נטל את הבנים – for since he took the fledglings, he had a designated–captive nest.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:4:1)


**לוֹקֶה וְאֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּחַ.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּלָאו הַנִּתָּק לַעֲשֵׂה אֵין לוֹקִין עָלָיו, הָכָא הַיְנוּ טַעֲמָא דְּסָבַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה שַׁלֵּחַ מֵעִקָּרָא מַשְׁמַע, וְהָכִי קָאָמַר קְרָא, לֹא תִקַּח הָאֵם, אֲבָל מַה יֵּשׁ עָלֶיךָ לַעֲשׂוֹת כְּשֶׁתִּמְצָא הַקֵּן, שַׁלֵּחַ תְּשַׁלַּח אֶת הָאֵם, וְאֵין כָּאן נִתָּק לַעֲשֵׂה אֶלָּא עֲבֵרַת עֲשֵׂה וְלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


לקה ואינו משלח – even though this Is a prohibition that after its violation is transformed into a positive commandment, flogging is not administered. Here this is the reason, as Rabbi Yehuda holds that sending away is implied from the outset, but what are you able to do when you find the nest? You send away the mother bird, and there is not here a negative prohibition that after its violation is transformed into a positive commandment, but rather, you have violated a positive commandment and a negative commandment, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:4:2)


**אֵין לוֹקִין עָלֶיהָ.** אִם קִיֵּם עֲשֵׂה שֶׁבָּהּ. אֲבָל אִם לֹא קִיֵּם הָעֲשֵׂה, כְּגוֹן הַלּוֹקֵחַ אֵם מֵעַל הַבָּנִים וּשְׁחָטָהּ אוֹ מֵתָה תַּחַת יָדוֹ, לוֹקֶה:


אין לוקין עליה – if he fulfilled the positive commandment that is in it, but if he did not fulfill the positive commandment, such as when one takes the mother from the fledglings and slaughters it, it dies while under his control, he is flogged.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Chullin 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Chullin/r/12:5:1)


**מִצְוָה שֶׁהִיא כְּאִסָּר.** שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ חֶסְרוֹן כִּיס אֶלָּא דָּבָר מֻעָט:


מצוה שהיא כאיסר – that there is no loss of money in it, other than a small amount.
